Now I have enough information to provide a comprehensive report. Let me compile the findings:

## Tool Definition Architecture - Comprehensive Report

Based on my thorough exploration of the codebase, here's a detailed mapping of tool definitions and their relationships across different layers:

### 1. CHAT AGENT TOOLS (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/app/src/server/chat/tools/`)

**Format:** Vercel AI SDK `tool()` function (via `ai` package)

**21 individual tool files** (excluding index.ts, types.ts, helpers.ts):
- `search-entities.ts` - Full-text BM25 search across knowledge graph
- `get-entity-detail.ts` - Fetch entity with relationships and provenance
- `list-workspace-entities.ts` - List entities by type/status
- `get-project-status.ts` - Project task/decision aggregation
- `get-conversation-history.ts` - Load recent conversation messages
- `create-work-item.ts` - Create task/feature/project (with authorization check)
- `suggest-work-items.ts` - Batch triage with dedup (>0.97 = exact, ≥0.8 = merge, <0.8 = new)
- `edit-work-item.ts` - Modify existing task/feature
- `move-items-to-project.ts` - Move work items between projects
- `create-provisional-decision.ts` - Draft decision for review
- `resolve-decision.ts` - Check if existing decision answers a question
- `confirm-decision.ts` - Finalize decision (requires authorization)
- `check-constraints.ts` - Validate against existing decisions/constraints
- `create-question.ts` - Create open question entity
- `create-observation.ts` - Log signal (contradiction/gap/pattern)
- `acknowledge-observation.ts` - Mark observation as reviewed
- `resolve-observation.ts` - Close resolved observation
- `create-suggestion.ts` - Proactive proposal for human review
- `invoke-pm-agent.ts` - Delegate to PM subagent
- `invoke-analytics-agent.ts` - Delegate to analytics subagent
- `show-relationship-graph.ts` - Visualize entity relationships

**Structure Pattern:**
```typescript
export function create[ToolName]Tool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
  return tool({
    description: "...",
    inputSchema: z.object({...}),
    execute: async (input, options) => { ... }
  });
}
```

**Dependencies:**
- `ChatToolDeps`: Contains `surreal`, `extractionModelId`, `extractionModel`, `extractionStoreThreshold`
- `ChatToolExecutionContext`: Actor type (`chat_agent | mcp | pm_agent | analytics_agent`), identity, workspace, conversation, message records
- Helper: `requireToolContext()` / `requireAuthorizedContext()` to extract context from AI SDK options

**Graph Queries Used:**
- `/app/src/server/graph/queries.ts` - Entity creation, detail fetching, relationships
- `/app/src/server/graph/bm25-search.ts` - Full-text search
- `/app/src/server/iam/authority.ts` - Authorization enforcement

---

### 2. AGENT TOOL COMPOSITION LAYERS

#### PM Agent (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/app/src/server/agents/pm/tools.ts`)
**Composes 8 chat tools** (subset for PM work):
```typescript
export function createPmTools(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
  return {
    search_entities,
    get_project_status,
    create_observation,
    create_suggestion,
    suggest_work_items,
    create_work_item,
    edit_work_item,
    move_items_to_project,
  };
}
```

#### Analytics Agent (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/app/src/server/agents/analytics/tools.ts`)
**Defines 1 custom tool** (NOT reused):
- `execute_analytics_query` - Raw SurrealQL SELECT queries with parameterization
  - Handler: Direct SurrealDB query execution + entity reference extraction
  - No authorization checks (read-only)

#### Observer Agent (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/app/src/server/agents/observer/tools.ts`)
**Composes 3 chat tools**:
```typescript
export function createObserverTools(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
  return {
    search_entities,
    get_entity_detail,
    create_observation,
  };
}
```

---

### 3. CLI MCP SERVER TOOLS (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/cli/mcp-server.ts`)

**Format:** MCP SDK `server.tool()` function (via `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk`)

**22 tools registered** across 3 tiers:

**Tier 1 (Read-only context):**
- `get_context` - Intent-based context resolution (primary)
- `get_workspace_context` - Workspace overview with hot items
- `get_project_context` - Full project state
- `get_task_context` - Task subgraph focus
- `get_active_decisions` - Grouped decisions by status
- `get_task_dependencies` - Dependency tree
- `get_architecture_constraints` - Confirmed decisions + observations
- `get_recent_changes` - Entity changes since timestamp
- `get_entity_detail` - Entity detail fetch
- `list_pending_suggestions` - Suggestions awaiting review

**Tier 2 (Reasoning):**
- `resolve_decision` - Check if existing decision answers question
- `check_constraints` - Validate proposed action

**Tier 3 (Write):**
- `create_provisional_decision` - Draft decision
- `ask_question` - Create question entity
- `update_task_status` - Modify task status
- `create_subtask` - Add subtask with semantic dedup
- `log_implementation_note` - Append implementation details
- `log_observation` - Codebase observation with category/severity
- `create_suggestion` - Proactive proposal
- `accept_suggestion` - Approve suggestion
- `dismiss_suggestion` - Reject suggestion
- `convert_suggestion` - Convert suggestion to entity

**Structure Pattern:**
```typescript
server.tool(
  "tool_name",
  "description",
  { param: z.string().describe("...") },
  async (input) => {
    const result = await client.someMethod(input);
    return { content: [{ type: "text", text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
  }
);
```

**Backend:** All MCP tools delegate to `BrainHttpClient` which makes HTTP requests to server endpoints

---

### 4. PROXY LAYER TOOL EXECUTION (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/app/src/server/proxy/`)

This is a separate tool execution path used in LLM request proxying:

**Files:**
- `tool-resolver.ts` - Resolves identity's effective toolset from `can_use` graph edges
- `tool-injector.ts` - Merges Brain tools into LLM request body
- `tool-router.ts` - Classifies tool_use blocks as `brain-native`, `integration`, or `unknown`
- `tool-executor.ts` - Executes brain-native tools locally (1 handler: `search_entities`)
- `tool-trace-writer.ts` - Records tool execution traces to graph

**Brain-native Tool Handler (in executor):**
- `search_entities` - Keyword search (CONTAINS) across tasks, decisions, features

**Key Difference:** Proxy tools use a **registry model** where tools are stored in SurrealDB (`mcp_tool` table) with:
- `name`, `description`, `input_schema`, `toolkit` (identifies "brain" vs integration)
- `status` (active/disabled)
- `can_use` edges granting tools to identities
- Credential provider integration for integration tools

---

### 5. TOOL REGISTRY INFRASTRUCTURE (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/app/src/server/tool-registry/`)

**Purpose:** Credential provider and account connection management

**Files:**
- `routes.ts` - HTTP routes for provider CRUD and account connection
- `types.ts` - Domain types (`CredentialProviderRecord`, `ConnectedAccountRecord`, etc.)
- `queries.ts` - SurrealDB queries for tool/provider/account operations
- `encryption.ts` - AES-256-GCM encryption for secrets
- `oauth-flow.ts` - OAuth2 state management and redirect URLs

**Not tool definitions themselves** but infrastructure for managing credentials used by integration tools

---

### 6. ACCEPTANCE TESTS (`/Users/marcus/conductor/workspaces/brain-v1/lahore-v1/tests/acceptance/tool-registry/`)

**Test Kit:** `tool-registry-test-kit.ts`
- Seed helpers for `mcp_tool` records
- Test APIs for tool CRUD, grants, account connection
- Driving ports document all HTTP endpoints

**Tests:**
- `01-tool-schema-and-grants.test.ts` - Tool definition validation
- `02-proxy-tool-injection.test.ts` - Tool injection into LLM requests
- `04-tool-call-tracing.test.ts` - Tool execution tracing
- `09-tool-governance.test.ts` - Authority/permission enforcement

---

### DUPLICATION SUMMARY

**No significant duplication exists**, but there is **deliberate composition**:

| Tool | Chat/Agents | MCP CLI | Proxy | Notes |
|------|------------|---------|-------|-------|
| search_entities | ✓ (AI SDK) | ✓ (HTTP) | ✓ (native handler) | Same semantic, 3 implementations |
| get_entity_detail | ✓ | ✓ | - | Reused in chat agents |
| create_observation | ✓ | - (via log_observation) | - | Chat tool only |
| create_provisional_decision | ✓ | ✓ | - | Duplicated in both layers |
| resolve_decision | ✓ | ✓ | - | Duplicated in both layers |
| check_constraints | ✓ | ✓ | - | Duplicated in both layers |

**Why this structure:**
1. **Chat tools** use Vercel AI SDK directly (for LLM tool calling)
2. **MCP tools** use MCP protocol (for coding agents via CLI)
3. **Proxy tools** use graph-native execution (for request interception)
4. **Composition** allows agents to reuse subsets (PM, Observer, Analytics agents)

---

     1→/**
     2→ * Tool Executor -- Effect Boundary
     3→ *
     4→ * Executes Brain-native tool calls via graph queries. This is the adapter/effect
     5→ * boundary -- it performs IO (SurrealDB queries) to fulfill tool calls that the
     6→ * pure tool-router classified as "brain-native".
     7→ *
     8→ * Each Brain-native tool handler is a function: (input, deps) -> ToolExecutionResult.
     9→ * Unknown tool names produce an error result (not an exception).
    10→ *
    11→ * Step 8.5 in the proxy pipeline.
    12→ */
    13→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    14→import type { ClassifiedToolCall } from "./tool-router";
    15→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";
    16→import { captureToolTrace } from "./tool-trace-writer";
    17→
    18→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→// Types
    20→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    21→
    22→/** Result of executing a single Brain-native tool call. */
    23→export type ToolExecutionResult = {
    24→  readonly toolUseId: string;
    25→  readonly content: string;
    26→  readonly isError: boolean;
    27→};
    28→
    29→/** Dependencies injected into tool executors. */
    30→export type ToolExecutorDeps = {
    31→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    32→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    33→  readonly identityId?: string;
    34→};
    35→
    36→/** Anthropic tool_result content block for follow-up requests. */
    37→export type ToolResultMessage = {
    38→  role: "user";
    39→  content: Array<{
    40→    type: "tool_result";
    41→    tool_use_id: string;
    42→    content: string;
    43→    is_error?: boolean;
    44→  }>;
    45→};
    46→
    47→/** Handler for a single Brain-native tool. */
    48→type BrainToolHandler = (
    49→  input: Record<string, unknown>,
    50→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
    51→) => Promise<ToolExecutionResult>;
    52→
    53→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→// Brain-Native Tool Handlers
    55→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    56→
    57→const searchEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
    58→  const query = typeof input.query === "string" ? input.query : "";
    59→
    60→  if (!query.trim()) {
    61→    return {
    62→      toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
    63→      content: JSON.stringify({ results: [], message: "Empty search query" }),
    64→      isError: false,
    65→    };
    66→  }
    67→
    68→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", deps.workspaceId);
    69→
    70→  // Simple keyword search across tasks, decisions, and features
    71→  // Uses basic string matching (CONTAINS) for the walking skeleton.
    72→  // Future: BM25 fulltext search.
    73→  type EntityRow = {
    74→    id: RecordId;
    75→    table: string;
    76→    title?: string;
    77→    summary?: string;
    78→    text?: string;
    79→    status?: string;
    80→  };
    81→
    82→  const results = await deps.surreal.query<[EntityRow[], EntityRow[], EntityRow[]]>(
    83→    `SELECT id, 'task' AS table, title, status FROM task WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
    84→     SELECT id, 'decision' AS table, summary, status FROM decision WHERE workspace = $ws AND (summary CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
    85→     SELECT id, 'feature' AS table, title, status FROM feature WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;`,
    86→    { ws: workspaceRecord, q: query },
    87→  );
    88→
    89→  const entities = [
    90→    ...(results[0] ?? []).map((row) => ({
    91→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
    92→      type: "task",
    93→      title: row.title ?? "",
    94→      status: row.status ?? "",
    95→    })),
    96→    ...(results[1] ?? []).map((row) => ({
    97→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
    98→      type: "decision",
    99→      title: row.summary ?? "",
   100→      status: row.status ?? "",
   101→    })),
   102→    ...(results[2] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   103→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   104→      type: "feature",
   105→      title: row.title ?? "",
   106→      status: row.status ?? "",
   107→    })),
   108→  ];
   109→
   110→  return {
   111→    toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
   112→    content: JSON.stringify({ results: entities, count: entities.length }),
   113→    isError: false,
   114→  };
   115→};
   116→
   117→/** Registry of Brain-native tool handlers, keyed by tool name. */
   118→const brainToolHandlers: ReadonlyMap<string, BrainToolHandler> = new Map([
   119→  ["search_entities", searchEntitiesHandler],
   120→]);
   121→
   122→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→// Effect Boundary: executeBrainNativeTools
   124→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   125→
   126→/**
   127→ * Execute all brain-native tool calls and return tool_result messages.
   128→ *
   129→ * - Each classified brain-native call is dispatched to its handler.
   130→ * - Unknown handlers produce an error result (graceful, not HTTP 500).
   131→ * - All errors are caught and returned as is_error tool_results.
   132→ */
   133→export async function executeBrainNativeTools(
   134→  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],
   135→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
   136→): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {
   137→  const brainNativeCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(
   138→    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: "brain-native" }> =>
   139→      c.classification === "brain-native",
   140→  );
   141→
   142→  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];
   143→
   144→  for (const call of brainNativeCalls) {
   145→    const handler = brainToolHandlers.get(call.toolUse.name);
   146→
   147→    if (!handler) {
   148→      results.push({
   149→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   150→        content: JSON.stringify({
   151→          error: `No handler registered for brain-native tool: ${call.toolUse.name}`,
   152→        }),
   153→        isError: true,
   154→      });
   155→      continue;
   156→    }
   157→
   158→    const startMs = performance.now();
   159→    try {
   160→      const result = await handler(call.toolUse.input, deps);
   161→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   162→      results.push({
   163→        ...result,
   164→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   165→      });
   166→
   167→      // Fire-and-forget trace capture (do not block tool result delivery)
   168→      captureToolTrace(
   169→        {
   170→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   171→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   172→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   173→          outcome: "success",
   174→          durationMs,
   175→          input: call.toolUse.input as Record<string, unknown>,
   176→          output: { content: result.content },
   177→        },
   178→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   179→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   180→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   181→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   182→          error: String(traceError),
   183→        });
   184→      });
   185→    } catch (error) {
   186→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   187→      log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.handler_error", "Brain-native tool execution failed", {
   188→        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   189→        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,
   190→        error: String(error),
   191→      });
   192→      results.push({
   193→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   194→        content: JSON.stringify({
   195→          error: `Tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,
   196→        }),
   197→        isError: true,
   198→      });
   199→
   200→      // Trace error outcome too
   201→      captureToolTrace(
   202→        {
   203→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   204→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   205→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   206→          outcome: "error",
   207→          durationMs,
   208→        },
   209→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   210→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   211→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   212→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   213→          error: String(traceError),
   214→        });
   215→      });
   216→    }
   217→  }
   218→
   219→  return results;
   220→}
   221→
   222→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   223→// Helper: buildToolResultMessages
   224→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   225→
   226→/**
   227→ * Build an Anthropic tool_result message from execution results.
   228→ * Used to construct the follow-up request in the tool use loop.
   229→ */
   230→export function buildToolResultMessage(
   231→  results: ToolExecutionResult[],
   232→): ToolResultMessage {
   233→  return {
   234→    role: "user",
   235→    content: results.map((result) => ({
   236→      type: "tool_result" as const,
   237→      tool_use_id: result.toolUseId,
   238→      content: result.content,
   239→      ...(result.isError ? { is_error: true } : {}),
   240→    })),
   241→  };
   242→}
   243→

    21→        .describe("Optional filter by entity kinds"),
    22→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(10).describe("Maximum number of results"),
    23→    }),
    24→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    25→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    26→
    27→      const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    28→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    29→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    30→        query: input.query,
    31→        ...(input.kinds ? { kinds: input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[] } : {}),
    32→        limit: input.limit,
    33→      });
    34→
    35→      const enriched = await Promise.all(
    36→        results.map(async (row) => {
    37→          const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);
    38→          const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({
    39→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    40→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    41→            entityRecord,
    42→            limit: 8,
    43→          });
    44→
    45→          return {
    46→            id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,
    47→            kind: row.kind,
    48→            name: row.name,
    49→            confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),
    50→            ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),
    51→            related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({
    52→              id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,
    53→              kind: neighbor.kind,
    54→              name: neighbor.name,
    55→              relation: neighbor.relationKind,
    56→            })),
    57→          };
    58→        }),
    59→      );
    60→
    61→      return {
    62→        query: input.query,
    63→        results: enriched,
    64→      };
    65→    },
    66→  });
    67→}
    68→

import { McpServer } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/mcp.js";
import { StdioServerTransport } from "@modelcontextprotocol/sdk/server/stdio.js";
import { z } from "zod";
import { requireConfig } from "./config";
import { BrainHttpClient } from "./http-client";

export async function runMcpServer(): Promise<void> {
  const config = await requireConfig();
  const client = new BrainHttpClient(config);

  const server = new McpServer({
    name: "brain",
    version: "0.1.0",
  });

  // =========================================================================
  // Primary — Intent-based context
  // =========================================================================

  server.tool(
    "get_context",
    "Load relevant knowledge graph context for your current work. Describe what you're doing and the server finds the right project, tasks, decisions, and constraints. Preferred over get_workspace_context / get_project_context / get_task_context.",
    {
      intent: z.string().describe("What you're working on. Can include task IDs (task:abc), project names, file paths, or a plain description of your goal."),
    },
    async (input) => {
      const result = await client.getContext({ intent: input.intent });
      return { content: [{ type: "text" as const, text: JSON.stringify(result, undefined, 2) }] };
    },
  );

Now I have a complete picture. Here's the **RPP Analysis**:

## Duplication Assessment

There are **three layers** implementing overlapping tool semantics:

| Layer | Format | Example: `search_entities` | Business Logic |
|-------|--------|---------------------------|----------------|
| **Chat tools** (`chat/tools/`) | Vercel AI SDK `tool()` | BM25 search + neighbor enrichment | Inline in tool `execute` |
| **CLI MCP** (`cli/mcp-server.ts`) | MCP SDK `server.tool()` | HTTP call → server endpoint → same queries | Delegated via `BrainHttpClient` |
| **Proxy executor** (`proxy/tool-executor.ts`) | Custom handler map | Simplified CONTAINS search | **Reimplemented** inline |

The architecture is actually mostly **correct by design**:

- **Chat tools** = Vercel AI SDK wrappers around graph queries (the **canonical business logic**)
- **CLI MCP tools** = thin HTTP proxies that call server MCP endpoints → these endpoints call the same graph queries
- **Proxy executor** = the **actual duplication problem** — it reimplements `search_entities` with a simplified CONTAINS query instead of reusing the existing BM25 search

The proxy executor (`tool-executor.ts:57-115`) has a **hand-rolled `search_entities`** that:
1. Uses basic `CONTAINS` instead of BM25 full-text search
2. Only searches 3 tables (task, decision, feature) vs the chat tool's 6 entity types
3. Has no neighbor enrichment
4. Returns a different shape

This is **L3 (Responsibilities)** — the proxy executor shouldn't own search implementation. It should delegate to the same graph query layer that chat tools use.

However, before I plan a full refactor, I want to clarify something with you:

**The user's request is about moving tool functions to a shared `tools/` directory.** The actual state is:

1. `chat/tools/` already serves as the shared tool layer — PM agent, Observer agent, and Analytics agent all compose from it
2. The CLI MCP server tools are **not duplicated** — they're thin HTTP clients calling server endpoints (correct architecture for a CLI that runs out-of-process)
3. The real duplication is **only in `proxy/tool-executor.ts`** — it reimplements `search_entities` instead of calling the existing BM25 search

Should I:
- **A)** Fix the proxy executor to delegate to the existing graph query layer (`graph/bm25-search.ts`) — this is the minimal fix for the actual duplication
- **B)** Move `chat/tools/` → `app/src/server/tools/` and have chat, agents, AND proxy all import from there — the structural rename you suggested
- **C)** Both A + B

Option B is mostly a rename (L1 readability) since chat/tools is already the shared layer. Option A fixes the actual business logic duplication (L3). What's your preference?

> DEVELOPER

C and then make more of the tools available to the proxy

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { getEntityDetail, parseRecordIdString, type GraphEntityTable } from "../../graph/queries";
     4→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     5→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     6→
     7→export function createGetEntityDetailTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
     8→  return tool({
     9→    description:
    10→      "Get full details about a specific entity including relationships, provenance, and related entities.",
    11→    inputSchema: z.object({
    12→      entityId: z.string().min(1).describe("Entity record ID, e.g. decision:abc123"),
    13→    }),
    14→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    15→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    16→      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(
    17→        input.entityId,
    18→        ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"],
    19→      ) as unknown as ReturnType<typeof parseRecordIdString<GraphEntityTable>>;
    20→
    21→      const detail = await getEntityDetail({
    22→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    23→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    24→        entityRecord,
    25→      });
    26→
    27→      return detail;
    28→    },
    29→  });
    30→}
    31→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import {
     5→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     6→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     7→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→} from "../../graph/queries";
    10→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../../observation/queries";
    11→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→const entityKindEnum = z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"]);
    15→
    16→export function createListWorkspaceEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    17→  return tool({
    18→    description:
    19→      "List workspace entities by kind. Use this to answer questions about what entities exist (e.g. \"what decisions are there?\", \"list open questions\", \"show all tasks\"). For semantic search by topic, use search_entities instead.",
    20→    inputSchema: z.object({
    21→      kind: entityKindEnum.describe("Entity kind to list."),
    22→      status: z.string().optional().describe("Optional status filter, e.g. 'provisional', 'confirmed', 'open', 'done'."),
    23→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project name or ID to scope results. Only use when the user explicitly mentions a project."),
    24→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(25).describe("Maximum number of results."),
    25→    }),
    26→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    27→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    28→
    29→      const projectRecord = input.project
    30→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    31→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    32→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    33→            projectInput: input.project,
    34→          })
    35→        : undefined;
    36→
    37→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    38→        const projects = await listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    39→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    40→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    41→          limit: input.limit,
    42→        });
    43→        return {
    44→          kind: "project",
    45→          count: projects.length,
    46→          entities: projects.map((p) => ({
    47→            id: `project:${p.id}`,
    48→            name: p.name,
    49→            activeTaskCount: p.activeTaskCount,
    50→          })),
    51→        };
    52→      }
    53→
    54→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    55→        return listTasks(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    56→      }
    57→
    58→      if (input.kind === "feature") {
    59→        return listFeatures(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    60→      }
    61→
    62→      if (input.kind === "decision") {
    63→        const decisions = await listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({
    64→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    65→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    66→          limit: input.limit,
    67→        });
    68→        const filtered = input.status
    69→          ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === input.status)
    70→          : decisions;
    71→        return {
    72→          kind: "decision",
    73→          count: filtered.length,
    74→          entities: filtered.map((d) => ({
    75→            id: `decision:${d.id}`,
    76→            name: d.name,
    77→            status: d.status,
    78→            ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),
    79→            ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),
    80→          })),
    81→        };
    82→      }
    83→
    84→      if (input.kind === "question") {
    85→        const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({
    86→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    87→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    88→          limit: input.limit,
    89→        });
    90→        return {
    91→          kind: "question",
    92→          count: questions.length,
    93→          entities: questions.map((q) => ({
    94→            id: `question:${q.id}`,
    95→            name: q.name,
    96→            ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),
    97→            ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),
    98→          })),
    99→        };
   100→      }
   101→
   102→      if (input.kind === "observation") {
   103→        const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
   104→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   105→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   106→          limit: input.limit,
   107→        });
   108→        return {
   109→          kind: "observation",
   110→          count: observations.length,
   111→          entities: observations.map((o) => ({
   112→            id: `observation:${o.id}`,
   113→            text: o.text,
   114→            severity: o.severity,
   115→            status: o.status,
   116→            ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),
   117→            sourceAgent: o.sourceAgent,
   118→          })),
   119→        };
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      throw new Error(`unsupported entity kind: ${input.kind}`);
   123→    },
   124→  });
   125→}
   126→
   127→async function listTasks(
   128→  surreal: Surreal,
   129→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   130→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   131→  status: string | undefined,
   132→  limit: number,
   133→) {
   134→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   135→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   136→
   137→  if (projectRecord) {
   138→    conditions.push(
   139→      "(id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)"
   140→      + " OR id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_task WHERE `in` IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)))",
   141→    );
   142→    params.project = projectRecord;
   143→  }
   144→
   145→  if (status) {
   146→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   147→    params.status = status;
   148→  }
   149→
   150→  const query = [
   151→    "SELECT id, title, status, priority, created_at",
   152→    "FROM task",
   153→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   154→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   155→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   156→  ].join(" ");
   157→
   158→  const [rows] = await surreal
   159→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   160→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   161→
   162→  return {
   163→    kind: "task",
   164→    count: rows.length,
   165→    entities: rows.map((t) => ({
   166→      id: `task:${(t.id.id as string)}`,
   167→      name: t.title,
   168→      status: t.status,
   169→      ...(t.priority ? { priority: t.priority } : {}),
   170→    })),
   171→  };
   172→}
   173→
   174→async function listFeatures(
   175→  surreal: Surreal,
   176→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   177→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   178→  status: string | undefined,
   179→  limit: number,
   180→) {
   181→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   182→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   183→
   184→  if (projectRecord) {
   185→    conditions.push("id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)");
   186→    params.project = projectRecord;
   187→  }
   188→
   189→  if (status) {
   190→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   191→    params.status = status;
   192→  }
   193→
   194→  const query = [
   195→    "SELECT id, name, status, created_at",
   196→    "FROM feature",
   197→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   198→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   199→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   200→  ].join(" ");
   201→
   202→  const [rows] = await surreal
   203→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   204→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   205→
   206→  return {
   207→    kind: "feature",
   208→    count: rows.length,
   209→    entities: rows.map((f) => ({
   210→      id: `feature:${(f.id.id as string)}`,
   211→      name: f.name,
   212→      status: f.status,
   213→    })),
   214→  };
   215→}
   216→

     1→import type { ToolExecutionOptions } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId } from "surrealdb";
     3→import type { ChatToolExecutionContext } from "./types";
     4→
     5→export function requireToolContext(options: ToolExecutionOptions): ChatToolExecutionContext {
     6→  const context = options.experimental_context as ChatToolExecutionContext | undefined;
     7→  if (!context) {
     8→    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is missing");
     9→  }
    10→
    11→  if (!context.workspaceRecord || !context.currentMessageRecord || !context.conversationRecord) {
    12→    throw new Error("chat tool execution context is malformed");
    13→  }
    14→
    15→  return context;
    16→}
    17→
    18→export function toDecisionRecordId(value: string): RecordId<"decision", string> {
    19→  const normalized = value.trim();
    20→  if (normalized.length === 0) {
    21→    throw new Error("decision id is required");
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  if (normalized.includes(":")) {
    25→    const [table, id] = normalized.split(":", 2);
    26→    if (table !== "decision" || !id) {
    27→      throw new Error("decision id must reference a decision record");
    28→    }
    29→    return new RecordId("decision", id);
    30→  }
    31→
    32→  return new RecordId("decision", normalized);
    33→}
    34→

```
     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../../graph/bm25-search";
     4→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     5→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     6→
     7→function normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {
     8→  return new Set(
     9→    value
    10→      .toLowerCase()
    11→      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
    12→      .split(/\s+/)
    13→      .map((token) => token.trim())
    14→      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),
    15→  );
    16→}
    17→
    18→function hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {
    19→  for (const value of a) {
    20→    if (b.has(value)) {
    21→      return true;
    22→    }
    23→  }
    24→
    25→  return false;
    26→}
    27→
    28→export function createCheckConstraintsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    29→  return tool({
    30→    description:
    31→      "Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.",
    32→    inputSchema: z.object({
    33→      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe("What is being proposed"),
    34→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    35→    }),
    36→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    37→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    38→
    39→      const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    40→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    41→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    42→        query: input.proposed_action,
    43→        kinds: ["decision"],
    44→        limit: 14,
    45→      });
    46→
    47→      const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(input.proposed_action);
    48→      const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    49→      const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    50→      const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    51→
    52→      for (const candidate of candidates) {
    53→        const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);
    54→        const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);
    55→        const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();
    56→
    57→        if (candidate.kind === "decision" && overlap && (status === "contested" || status === "superseded")) {
    58→          hardConflicts.push({
    59→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    60→            name: candidate.name,
    61→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    62→            reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,
    63→          });
    64→          continue;
    65→        }
    66→
    67→        if (candidate.kind === "decision" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {
    68→          supporting.push({
    69→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    70→            name: candidate.name,
    71→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    72→            reason: "High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.",
    73→          });
    74→          continue;
    75→        }
    76→
    77→        if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {
    78→          softTensions.push({
    79→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    80→            name: candidate.name,
    81→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    82→            reason: overlap
    83→              ? "Related decision/question may require consistency checks."
    84→              : "Semantically related context may be affected.",
    85→          });
    86→        }
    87→      }
    88→
    89→      return {
    90→        hard_conflicts: hardConflicts,
    91→        soft_tensions: softTensions,
    92→        supporting,
    93→        proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0,
    94→      };
    95→    },
    96→  });
    97→}
    98→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import {
     4→  createDecisionRecord,
     5→  createExtractionProvenanceEdge,
     6→  getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage,
     7→  parseRecordIdString,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord,
     9→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
    10→  type GraphEntityRecord,
    11→} from "../graph/queries";
    12→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
    13→import { requireAuthorizedContext } from "../iam/authority";
    14→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    15→
    16→const resolveDecisionInputSchema = z.object({
    17→  question: z.string().min(1).describe("The decision question"),
    18→  options: z.array(z.string().min(1)).optional().describe("Known options being considered"),
    19→  context: z
    20→    .object({
    21→      project: z.string().optional(),
    22→      feature: z.string().optional(),
    23→    })
    24→    .optional()
    25→    .describe("Optional project/feature scope for searching decisions"),
    26→});
    27→
    28→export function createResolveDecisionTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    29→  return tool({
    30→    description:
    31→      "Infer an answer to a decision question from graph context. Returns resolved/inferred/unresolved with rationale and sources.",
    32→    inputSchema: resolveDecisionInputSchema,
    33→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    34→      const { context } = await requireAuthorizedContext(options, "create_decision", deps);
    35→
    36→      const projectRecord = input.context?.project
    37→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    38→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    39→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    40→            projectInput: input.context.project,
    41→          })
    42→        : undefined;
    43→
    44→      const featureRecord = input.context?.feature
    45→        ? await resolveWorkspaceFeatureRecord({
    46→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    47→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    48→            featureInput: input.context.feature,
    49→          })
    50→        : undefined;
    51→
    52→      const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    53→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    54→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    55→        query: input.question,
    56→        kinds: ["decision"],
    57→        limit: 10,
    58→      });
    59→
    60→      const decisionCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "decision");
    61→      const topDecision = decisionCandidates[0];
    62→
    63→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.92) {
    64→        const existingDecision = parseRecordIdString(topDecision.id, ["decision"], "decision");
    65→        const resolvedFrom = await getDecisionPrimarySourceMessage({
    66→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    67→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    68→          decisionRecord: existingDecision,
    69→        });
    70→
    71→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
    72→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    73→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    74→          targetRecord: existingDecision,
    75→          now: new Date(),
    76→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
    77→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
    78→          fromText: input.question,
    79→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
    80→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
    81→          ...(resolvedFrom ? { resolvedFromRecord: resolvedFrom } : {}),
    82→        });
    83→
    84→        return {
    85→          status: "resolved",
    86→          decision: {
    87→            id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
    88→            name: topDecision.name,
    89→          },
    90→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    91→          sources: [
    92→            {
    93→              id: `decision:${topDecision.id}`,
    94→              name: topDecision.name,
    95→              score: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
    96→            },
    97→          ],
    98→        };
    99→      }
   100→
   101→      if (topDecision && topDecision.score >= 0.78) {
   102→        const now = new Date();
   103→        const sources = candidates.slice(0, 5);
   104→        const basedOn = sources
   105→          .map((source) => {
   106→            try {
   107→              return parseRecordIdString(source.id, [source.kind as "project" | "feature" | "task" | "decision" | "question"], source.kind as any);
   108→            } catch {
   109→              return undefined;
   110→            }
   111→          })
   112→          .filter((value): value is GraphEntityRecord => value !== undefined);
   113→
   114→        const decisionRecord = await createDecisionRecord({
   115→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   116→          summary: input.question,
   117→          status: "inferred",
   118→          now,
   119→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   120→          sourceMessageRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   121→          rationale: `Inferred from existing graph context with ${sources.length} related entities.`,
   122→          ...(input.options && input.options.length > 0 ? { optionsConsidered: input.options } : {}),
   123→          ...(basedOn.length > 0 ? { basedOn } : {}),
   124→          inferredBy: "chat_agent",
   125→          decidedByName: "chat_agent",
   126→          ...(projectRecord ? { projectRecord } : {}),
   127→          ...(featureRecord ? { featureRecord } : {}),
   128→        });
   129→
   130→        await createExtractionProvenanceEdge({
   131→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   132→          sourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   133→          targetRecord: decisionRecord,
   134→          now,
   135→          confidence: Math.max(topDecision.score, 0.6),
   136→          model: deps.extractionModelId,
   137→          fromText: input.question,
   138→          evidence: context.latestUserText,
   139→          evidenceSourceRecord: context.currentMessageRecord,
   140→        });
   141→
   142→        return {
   143→          status: "inferred",
   144→          decision: {
   145→            id: `decision:${decisionRecord.id as string}`,
   146→            name: input.question,
   147→          },
   148→          confidence: Number(topDecision.score.toFixed(4)),
   149→          rationale: `Top matching decision: ${topDecision.name}. Related context was strong enough to infer a recommendation.`,
   150→          sources: sources.map((source) => ({
   151→            id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   152→            name: source.name,
   153→            score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   154→          })),
   155→        };
   156→      }
   157→
   158→      return {
   159→        status: "unresolved",
   160→        relatedContext: candidates.slice(0, 6).map((source) => ({
   161→          id: `${source.kind}:${source.id}`,
   162→          kind: source.kind,
   163→          name: source.name,
   164→          score: Number(source.score.toFixed(4)),
   165→        })),
   166→        suggestProvisional: true,
   167→      };
   168→    },
   169→  });
   170→}
   171→
```

input.entityId : \"\";\n  if (!entityId.trim()) return err(\"entityId is required\");\n\n  const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(\n    entityId,\n    [\"workspace\", \"project\", \"person\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"decision\", \"question\"],\n  ) as unknown as ReturnType<typeof parseRecordIdString<GraphEntityTable>>;\n\n  const detail = await getEntityDetail({\n    surreal: deps.surreal,\n    workspaceRecord: workspaceRecord(deps),\n    entityRecord,\n  });\n\n  return ok(detail);\n};\n\nconst getProjectStatusHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const projectId = typeof input.projectId === \"string\" ? input.status : undefined;\n    const filtered = status ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === status) : decisions;\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"decision\",\n      count: filtered.length,\n      entities: filtered.map((d) => ({\n        id: `decision:${d.id}`,\n        name: d.name,\n        status: d.status,\n        ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),\n        ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"question\") {\n    const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({\n      surreal: deps.surreal,\n      workspaceRecord: wsRecord,\n      limit,\n    });\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"question\",\n      count: questions.length,\n      entities: questions.map((q) => ({\n        id: `question:${q.id}`,\n        name: q.name,\n        ...(q.priority ? name. */\nconst brainToolHandlers: ReadonlyMap<string, BrainToolHandler> = new Map([\n  [\"search_entities\", searchEntitiesHandler],\n  [\"get_entity_detail\", getEntityDetailHandler],\n executeBrainNativeTools(\n  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],\n  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,\n): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {\n  const brainNativeCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(\n    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: \"brain-native\" }> =>\n      c.classification === \"brain-native\",\n  );\n\n  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];\n\n  for (const call of toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n      });\n\n      // Fire-and-forget trace capture (do not block tool result delivery)\n      captureToolTrace(\n        {\n          toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n log.warn(\"proxy.tool_executor.handler_error\", \"Brain-native tool execution failed\", {\n        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,\n        error: String(error),\n      });\n      results.push({\n        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n        content: JSON.stringify({\n          error: `Tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,\n        }),\n        isError: true,\n      });\n\n      // Trace error outcome too\n      captureToolTrace(\n        {\n          toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n          identityId: deps.identityId,\n          outcome: \"error\",\n          durationMs,\n        },\n        { surreal: deps.surreal },\n      ).catch((traceError) => {\n        log.warn(\"proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed\", \"Tool trace capture failed\", {\n          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n          error: String(traceError),\n        });\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n    142→export async function executeBrainNativeTools(

   212→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   213→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   214→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   215→          outcome: "error",
   216→          durationMs,
   217→        },
   218→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   219→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   220→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   221→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   222→          error: String(traceError),
   223→        });
   224→      });
   225→    }
   226→  }
   227→
   228→  return results;
   229→}
   230→
   231→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   232→// Helper: buildToolResultMessages
   233→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   234→
   235→/**
   236→ * Build an Anthropic tool_result message from execution results.
   237→ * Used to construct the follow-up request in the tool use loop.
   238→ */
   239→export function buildToolResultMessage(
   240→  results: ToolExecutionResult[],
   241→): ToolResultMessage {
   242→  return {
   243→    role: "user",
   244→    content: results.map((result) => ({
   245→      type: "tool_result" as const,
   246→      tool_use_id: result.toolUseId,
   247→      content: result.content,
   248→      ...(result.isError ? { is_error: true } : {}),
   249→    })),
   250→  };
   251→}
   252→
   253→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   254→// Response Sanitization (Pure Functions)
   255→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→
   257→/** Headers that must be stripped from integration HTTP responses. */
   258→const SENSITIVE_HEADERS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
   259→  "authorization",
   260→  "set-cookie",
   261→  "x-api-key",
   262→  "www-authenticate",
   263→]);
   264→
   265→/** JSON field names (lowercase) that must be recursively stripped from response bodies. */
   266→const SENSITIVE_FIELDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
   267→  "access_token",
   268→  "refresh_token",
   269→  "api_key",
   270→  "password",
   271→  "client_secret",
   272→  "bearer_token",
   273→]);
   274→
   275→/** Maximum response body size in bytes before truncation. */
   276→const MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 100 * 1024; // 100KB
   277→
   278→/**
   279→ * Strip sensitive headers from an HTTP response headers map.
   280→ * Pure function: returns a new record with sensitive headers removed.
   281→ */
   282→export function sanitizeResponseHeaders(
   283→  headers: Record<string, string>,
   284→): Record<string, string> {
   285→  const sanitized: Record<string, string> = {};
   286→  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(headers)) {
   287→    if (!SENSITIVE_HEADERS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
   288→      sanitized[key] = value;
   289→    }
   290→  }
   291→  return sanitized;
   292→}
   293→
   294→/**
   295→ * Recursively strip sensitive fields from a JSON value.
   296→ * Pure function: returns a new value with credential fields removed.
   297→ */
   298→export function sanitizeResponseBody(value: unknown): unknown {
   299→  if (value === null || value === undefined) return value;
   300→
   301→  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   302→    return value.map(sanitizeResponseBody);
   303→  }
   304→
   305→  if (typeof value === "object") {
   306→    const sanitized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   307→    for (const [key, fieldValue] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
   308→      if (!SENSITIVE_FIELDS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
   309→        sanitized[key] = sanitizeResponseBody(fieldValue);
   310→      }
   311→    }
   312→    return sanitized;
   313→  }
   314→
   315→  return value;
   316→}
   317→
   318→/**
   319→ * Truncate a string to the maximum allowed byte size.
   320→ * Pure function: appends a truncation marker if the content exceeds the limit.
   321→ */
   322→export function truncateResponse(content: string): string {
   323→  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
   324→  const bytes = encoder.encode(content);
   325→  if (bytes.length <= MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) return content;
   326→
   327→  const truncated = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(0, MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES));
   328→  return `${truncated}\n\n[Response truncated: ${bytes.length} bytes exceeds ${MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES} byte limit]`;
   329→}
   330→
   331→/**
   332→ * Full sanitization pipeline for integration tool responses.
   333→ * Pure function: sanitize body -> truncate -> return content string.
   334→ */
   335→export function sanitizeIntegrationResponse(
   336→  responseBody: string,
   337→  responseHeaders: Record<string, string>,
   338→): { content: string; sanitizedHeaders: Record<string, string> } {
   339→  const sanitizedHeaders = sanitizeResponseHeaders(responseHeaders);
   340→
   341→  // Try to parse and sanitize JSON body
   342→  let sanitizedContent: string;
   343→  try {
   344→    const parsed = JSON.parse(responseBody);
   345→    const sanitized = sanitizeResponseBody(parsed);
   346→    sanitizedContent = JSON.stringify(sanitized);
   347→  } catch {
   348→    // Not JSON -- use raw body
   349→    sanitizedContent = responseBody;
   350→  }
   351→
   352→  const truncatedContent = truncateResponse(sanitizedContent);
   353→
   354→  return { content: truncatedContent, sanitizedHeaders };
   355→}
   356→
   357→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   358→// Effect Boundary: executeIntegrationTools
   359→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   360→
   361→/**
   362→ * Execute all integration tool calls via HTTP with brokered credentials.
   363→ *
   364→ * For each integration tool call:
   365→ *   1. Resolve credentials via credential-resolver
   366→ *   2. Execute HTTP call to integration API
   367→ *   3. Sanitize response (strip auth headers + credential fields)
   368→ *   4. Truncate to 100KB
   369→ *   5. Return as tool_result
   370→ *   6. Write trace record
   371→ *
   372→ * Errors are returned as is_error tool_results, never thrown as exceptions.
   373→ */
   374→export async function executeIntegrationTools(
   375→  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],
   376→  deps: IntegrationExecutorDeps,
   377→): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {
   378→  const integrationCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(
   379→    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: "integration" }> =>
   380→      c.classification === "integration",
   381→  );
   382→
   383→  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];
   384→
   385→  for (const call of integrationCalls) {
   386→    const startMs = performance.now();
   387→    const providerRef = call.resolvedTool.toolkit;
   388→
   389→    try {
   390→      // Step 1: Resolve credentials
   391→      if (!deps.identityId) {
   392→        results.push({
   393→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   394→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: "Identity required for integration tool calls" }),
   395→          isError: true,
   396→        });
   397→        continue;
   398→      }
   399→
   400→      const credentialResolverDeps: CredentialResolverDeps = {
   401→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   402→        toolEncryptionKey: deps.toolEncryptionKey,
   403→      };
   404→
   405→      const credResult = await resolveCredentialsForTool(
   406→        call.toolUse.name,
   407→        deps.identityId,
   408→        credentialResolverDeps,
   409→      );
   410→
   411→      if (!credResult.ok) {
   412→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   413→        results.push({
   414→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   415→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: `Credential resolution failed: ${credResult.error}` }),
   416→          isError: true,
   417→        });
   418→
   419→        captureToolTrace(
   420→          {
   421→            toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   422→            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   423→            identityId: deps.identityId,
   424→            outcome: "error",
   425→            durationMs,
   426→            input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   427→          },
   428→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   429→        ).catch((traceError) => {
   430→          log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   431→            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   432→            error: String(traceError),
   433→          });
   434→        });
   435→        continue;
   436→      }
   437→
   438→      // Step 2: Build and execute HTTP request
   439→      // The tool input should contain the endpoint URL or we derive it from provider config
   440→      const endpointUrl = typeof call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url === "string"
   441→        ? call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url
   442→        : typeof call.toolUse.input.url === "string"
   443→          ? call.toolUse.input.url
   444→          : undefined;
   445→
   446→      if (!endpointUrl) {
   447→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   448→        results.push({
   449→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   450→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: "Integration tool call missing endpoint_url or url in input" }),
   451→          isError: true,
   452→        });
   453→
   454→        captureToolTrace(
   455→          {
   456→            toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   457→            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   458→            identityId: deps.identityId,
   459→            outcome: "error",
   460→            durationMs,
   461→            input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   462→          },
   463→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   464→        ).catch((traceError) => {
   465→          log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   466→            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   467→            error: String(traceError),
   468→          });
   469→        });
   470→        continue;
   471→      }
   472→
   473→      const httpMethod = typeof call.toolUse.input.method === "string"
   474→        ? call.toolUse.input.method.toUpperCase()
   475→        : "POST";
   476→
   477→      const requestHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
   478→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   479→        ...credResult.headers,
   480→      };
   481→
   482→      // Build request body from tool input (excluding meta fields)
   483→      const { endpoint_url: _eu, url: _u, method: _m, ...bodyInput } = call.toolUse.input;
   484→      const hasBody = httpMethod !== "GET" && httpMethod !== "HEAD" && Object.keys(bodyInput).length > 0;
   485→
   486→      const fetchOptions: RequestInit = {
   487→        method: httpMethod,
   488→        headers: requestHeaders,
   489→        ...(hasBody ? { body: JSON.stringify(bodyInput) } : {}),
   490→      };
   491→
   492→      const httpResponse = await fetch(endpointUrl, fetchOptions);
   493→      const responseBody = await httpResponse.text();
   494→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   495→
   496→      // Step 3-4: Sanitize and truncate
   497→      const responseHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
   498→      httpResponse.headers.forEach((value, key) => {
   499→        responseHeaders[key] = value;
   500→      });
   501→
   502→      const { content } = sanitizeIntegrationResponse(responseBody, responseHeaders);
   503→
   504→      // Step 5: Return as tool_result
   505→      const isError = httpResponse.status >= 400;
   506→      const resultContent = isError
   507→        ? JSON.stringify({
   508→            error: `Integration API returned ${httpResponse.status}`,
   509→            status: httpResponse.status,
   510→            body: content,
   511→          })
   512→        : content;
   513→
   514→      results.push({
   515→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   516→        content: resultContent,
   517→        isError,
   518→      });
   519→
   520→      // Step 6: Write trace record
   521→      captureToolTrace(
   522→        {
   523→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   524→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   525→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   526→          outcome: isError ? "error" : "success",
   527→          durationMs,
   528→          input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   529→          output: { status: httpResponse.status, content_length: content.length },
   530→        },
   531→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   532→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   533→        log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   534→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   535→          error: String(traceError),
   536→        });
   537→      });
   538→    } catch (error) {
   539→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   540→
   541→      // Integration errors are tool_result errors, not HTTP 500s
   542→      log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.error", "Integration tool execution failed", {
   543→        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   544→        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,
   545→        error: String(error),
   546→      });
   547→
   548→      results.push({
   549→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   550→        content: JSON.stringify({
   551→          error: `Integration tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,
   552→        }),
   553→        isError: true,
   554→      });
   555→
   556→      captureToolTrace(
   557→        {
   558→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   559→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   560→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   561→          outcome: "error",
   562→          durationMs,
   563→          input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   564→        },
   565→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   566→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   567→        log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   568→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   569→          error: String(traceError),
   570→        });
   571→      });
   572→    }
   573→  }
   574→
   575→  return results;
   576→}
   577→

{ priority: q.priority } : {}),\n        ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"observation\") {\n    const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({ surreal: deps.surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, limit });\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"observation\",\n      count: observations.length,\n      entities: observations.map((o) => ({\n        id: `observation:${o.id}`, text: o.text, severity: o.severity, status: o.status,\n        ...(o.category ?      1→/**
     2→ * Tool Executor -- Effect Boundary
     3→ *
     4→ * Executes Brain-native tool calls via graph queries. This is the adapter/effect
     5→ * boundary -- it performs IO (SurrealDB queries) to fulfill tool calls that the
     6→ * pure tool-router classified as "brain-native".
     7→ *
     8→ * Each Brain-native tool handler is a function: (input, deps) -> ToolExecutionResult.
     9→ * Unknown tool names produce an error result (not an exception).
    10→ *
    11→ * Step 8.5 in the proxy pipeline.
    12→ */
    13→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    14→import type { ClassifiedToolCall } from "./tool-router";
    15→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";

    17→import {
    18→  resolveCredentialsForTool,
    19→  type CredentialResolverDeps,
    20→} from "./credential-resolver";
    21→
    22→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→// Types
    24→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→
    26→/** Result of executing a single Brain-native tool call. */
    27→export type ToolExecutionResult = {
    28→  readonly toolUseId: string;
    29→  readonly content: string;
    30→  readonly isError: boolean;
    31→};
    32→
    33→/** Dependencies injected into tool executors. */
    34→export type ToolExecutorDeps = {
    35→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    36→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    37→  readonly identityId?: string;
    38→};
    39→
    40→/** Dependencies for integration tool execution (extends base with credential resolver). */
    41→export type IntegrationExecutorDeps = ToolExecutorDeps & {
    42→  readonly toolEncryptionKey: string;
    43→};
    44→
    45→/** Anthropic tool_result content block for follow-up requests. */
    46→export type ToolResultMessage = {
    47→  role: "user";
    48→  content: Array<{
    49→    type: "tool_result";
    50→    tool_use_id: string;
    51→    content: string;
    52→    is_error?: boolean;
    53→  }>;
    54→};
    55→
    56→/** Handler for a single Brain-native tool. */
    57→type BrainToolHandler = (
    58→  input: Record<string, unknown>,
    59→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
    60→) => Promise<ToolExecutionResult>;
    61→
    62→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→// Brain-Native Tool Handlers
    64→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→
    66→const searchEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
    67→  const query = typeof input.query === "string" ? input.query : "";
    68→
    69→  if (!query.trim()) {
    70→    return {
    71→      toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
    72→      content: JSON.stringify({ results: [], message: "Empty search query" }),
    73→      isError: false,
    74→    };
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", deps.workspaceId);
    78→
    79→  // Simple keyword search across tasks, decisions, and features
    80→  // Uses basic string matching (CONTAINS) for the walking skeleton.
    81→  // Future: BM25 fulltext search.
    82→  type EntityRow = {
    83→    id: RecordId;
    84→    table: string;
    85→    title?: string;
    86→    summary?: string;
    87→    text?: string;
    88→    status?: string;
    89→  };
    90→
    91→  const results = await deps.surreal.query<[EntityRow[], EntityRow[], EntityRow[]]>(
    92→    `SELECT id, 'task' AS table, title, status FROM task WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
    93→     SELECT id, 'decision' AS table, summary, status FROM decision WHERE workspace = $ws AND (summary CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
    94→     SELECT id, 'feature' AS table, title, status FROM feature WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;`,
    95→    { ws: workspaceRecord, q: query },
    96→  );
    97→
    98→  const entities = [
    99→    ...(results[0] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   100→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   101→      type: "task",
   102→      title: row.title ?? "",
   103→      status: row.status ?? "",
   104→    })),
   105→    ...(results[1] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   106→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   107→      type: "decision",
   108→      title: row.summary ?? "",
   109→      status: row.status ?? "",
   110→    })),
   111→    ...(results[2] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   112→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   113→      type: "feature",
   114→      title: row.title ?? "",
   115→      status: row.status ?? "",
   116→    })),
   117→  ];
   118→
   119→  return {
   120→    toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
   121→    content: JSON.stringify({ results: entities, count: entities.length }),
   122→    isError: false,
   123→  };

   125→
   126→/** Registry of Brain-native tool handlers, keyed by tool name. */
   127→const brainToolHandlers: ReadonlyMap<string, BrainToolHandler> = new Map([

   129→]);
   130→
   131→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   132→// Effect Boundary: executeBrainNativeTools
   133→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   134→
   135→/**
   136→ * Execute all brain-native tool calls and return tool_result messages.
   137→ *
   138→ * - Each classified brain-native call is dispatched to its handler.
   139→ * - Unknown handlers produce an error result (graceful, not HTTP 500).
   140→ * - All errors are caught and returned as is_error tool_results.

  "new_string": "import { RecordId, type Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\nimport type { ClassifiedToolCall } from \"./tool-router\";\nimport { log } from \"../telemetry/logger\";\nimport {
