\"../graph/bm25-search\";\nimport {\n  getEntityDetail,\n  getProjectStatus,\n  listEntityNeighbors,\n  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,\n  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,\n  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,\n  parseRecordIdString,\n  type GraphEntityTable,\n  type SearchEntityKind,\n} from \"../graph/queries\";\nimport { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from \"../observation/queries\";\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Types\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Result of executing a single Brain-native tool call. */\nexport type ToolExecutionResult = {\n  readonly toolUseId: string;\n  readonly content: string;\n  readonly isError: boolean;\n};\n\n/** Dependencies injected into tool executors. */\nexport type ToolExecutorDeps = {\n  readonly surreal: Surreal;\n  readonly workspaceId: string;\n  readonly identityId?: string;\n};\n\n/** Dependencies for integration tool execution (extends base with credential resolver). */\nexport type IntegrationExecutorDeps = ToolExecutorDeps & {\n  readonly toolEncryptionKey: string;\n};\n\n/** Anthropic tool_result content block for follow-up requests. */\nexport type ToolResultMessage = {\n  role: \"user\";\n  content: Array<{\n    type: \"tool_result\";\n    tool_use_id: string;\n    content: string;\n    is_error?: boolean;\n  }>;\n};\n\n/** Handler for a single Brain-native tool. */\ntype BrainToolHandler = (\n  input: Record<string, unknown>,\n  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,\n) => Promise<ToolExecutionResult>;\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helpers\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nfunction wsRecord(deps: ToolExecutorDeps): RecordId<\"workspace\", string> {\n  return new RecordId(\"workspace\", deps.workspaceId);\n}\n\nfunction ok(content: unknown): ToolExecutionResult {\n  return { toolUseId: \"\", content: JSON.stringify(content), isError: false };\n}\n\nfunction fail(message: string): ToolExecutionResult {\n  return { toolUseId: \"\", content: JSON.stringify({ error: message }), isError: true };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Brain-Native Tool Handlers\n//\n// All handlers delegate to the shared graph query layer (graph/, observation/)\n// rather than reimplementing queries inline.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nconst searchEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const query = typeof input.query === \"string\" ? input.query : \"\";\n  if (!query.trim()) return ok({ results: [], message: \"Empty search query\" });\n\n  const kinds = Array.isArray(input.kinds) ? (input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[]) : undefined;\n  const limit = typeof input.limit === \"number\" ? Math.min(input.limit, 25) : 10;\n\n  const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n    surreal: deps.surreal,\n    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),\n    query,\n    ...(kinds ? { kinds } : {}),\n    limit,\n  });\n\n  const enriched = await Promise.all(\n    results.map(async (row) => {\n      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);\n      const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({\n        surreal: deps.surreal,\n        workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),\n        entityRecord,\n        limit: 8,\n      });\n\n      return {\n        id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,\n        kind: row.kind,\n        name: row.name,\n        confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),\n        ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),\n        related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({\n          id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,\n          kind: neighbor.kind,\n          name: neighbor.name,\n          relation: neighbor.relationKind,\n        })),\n      };\n    }),\n  );\n\n  return ok({ query, results: enriched });\n};\n\nconst getEntityDetailHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const entityId = typeof input.entityId === \"string\" ? input.entityId : \"\";\n  if (!entityId.trim()) return fail(\"entityId is required\");\n\n  const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(\n    entityId,\n    [\"workspace\", \"project\", \"person\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"decision\", \"question\"],\n  ) as unknown as ReturnType<typeof parseRecordIdString<GraphEntityTable>>;\n\n  const detail = await getEntityDetail({\n    surreal: deps.surreal,\n    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),\n    entityRecord,\n  });\n\n  return ok(detail);\n};\n\nconst getProjectStatusHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const projectId = typeof input.projectId === \"string\" ? input.projectId : \"\";\n  if (!projectId.trim()) return fail(\"projectId is required\");\n\n  const status = await getProjectStatus({\n    surreal: deps.surreal,\n    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),\n    projectInput: projectId,\n  });\n\n  return ok(status);\n};\n\nconst listWorkspaceEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const kind = typeof input.kind === \"string\" ? input.kind : \"\";\n  const limit = typeof input.limit === \"number\" ? Math.min(input.limit, 50) : 25;\n  const ws = wsRecord(deps);\n\n  if (kind === \"project\") {\n    const projects = await listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({ surreal: deps.surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, limit });\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"project\",\n      count: projects.length,\n      entities: projects.map((p) => ({ id: `project:${p.id}`, name: p.name, activeTaskCount: p.activeTaskCount })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"decision\") {\n    const decisions = await listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({ surreal: deps.surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, limit });\n    const status = typeof input.status === \"string\" ? input.status : undefined;\n    const filtered = status ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === status) : decisions;\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"decision\",\n      count: filtered.length,\n      entities: filtered.map((d) => ({\n        id: `decision:${d.id}`, name: d.name, status: d.status,\n        ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),\n        ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"question\") {\n    const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({ surreal: deps.surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, limit });\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"question\",\n      count: questions.length,\n      entities: questions.map((q) => ({\n        id: `question:${q.id}`, name: q.name,\n        ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),\n        ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"observation\") {\n    const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({ surreal: deps.surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, limit });\n    return ok({\n      kind: \"observation\",\n      count: observations.length,\n      entities: observations.map((o) => ({\n        id: `observation:${o.id}`, text: o.text, severity: o.severity, status: o.status,\n        ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),\n        sourceAgent: o.sourceAgent,\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (kind === \"task\" || kind === \"feature\") {\n    const nameField = kind === \"task\" ? \"title\" : \"name\";\n    const [rows] = await deps.surreal.query<[Array<{ id: RecordId; [key: string]: unknown }>]>(\n      `SELECT id, ${nameField}, status, created_at FROM ${kind} WHERE workspace = $ws ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT $limit;`,\n      { ws, limit },\n    );\n    return ok({\n      kind,\n      count: (rows ?? []).length,\n      entities: (rows ?? []).map((row) => ({\n        id: `${kind}:${(row.id as RecordId).id as string}`,\n        name: (row[nameField] as string) ?? \"\",\n        status: (row.status as string) ?? \"\",\n      })),\n    });\n  }\n\n  return fail(`Unsupported entity kind: ${kind}. Use: project, feature, task, decision, question, observation`);\n};\n\n/** Token normalization for constraint checking — mirrors logic in tools/check-constraints.ts. */\nfunction normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {\n  return new Set(\n    value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\\s]/g, \" \").split(/\\s+/)\n      .map((t) => t.trim()).filter((t) => t.length > 2),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {\n  for (const value of a) {\n    if (b.has(value)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\nconst checkConstraintsHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const proposedAction = typeof input.proposed_action === \"string\" ? input.proposed_action : \"\";\n  if (!proposedAction.trim()) return fail(\"proposed_action is required\");\n\n  const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n    surreal: deps.surreal,\n    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),\n    query: proposedAction,\n    kinds: [\"decision\"],\n    limit: 14,\n  });\n\n  const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(proposedAction);\n  const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n  const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n  const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n\n  for (const candidate of candidates) {\n    const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);\n    const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);\n    const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();\n\n    if (candidate.kind === \"decision\" && overlap && (status === \"contested\" || status === \"superseded\")) {\n      hardConflicts.push({\n        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,\n        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)), reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (candidate.kind === \"decision\" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {\n      supporting.push({\n        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,\n        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)), reason: \"High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.\",\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {\n      softTensions.push({\n        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,\n        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n        reason: overlap\n          ?      4→ * Executes Brain-native tool calls via graph queries. This is the adapter/effect
     5→ * boundary -- it performs IO (SurrealDB queries) to fulfill tool calls that the
     6→ * pure tool-router classified as "brain-native".
     7→ *
     8→ * Each Brain-native tool handler is a function: (input, deps) -> ToolExecutionResult.
     9→ * Unknown tool names produce an error result (not an exception).
    10→ *
    11→ * Step 8.5 in the proxy pipeline.
    12→ */
    13→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    14→import type { ClassifiedToolCall } from "./tool-router";
    15→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";
    16→import { captureToolTrace } from "./tool-trace-writer";
    17→import {
    18→  resolveCredentialsForTool,
    19→  type CredentialResolverDeps,
    20→} from "./credential-resolver";
    21→
    22→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    23→// Types
    24→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→
    26→/** Result of executing a single Brain-native tool call. */
    27→export type ToolExecutionResult = {
    28→  readonly toolUseId: string;
    29→  readonly content: string;
    30→  readonly isError: boolean;
    31→};
    32→
    33→/** Dependencies injected into tool executors. */
    34→export type ToolExecutorDeps = {
    35→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    36→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    37→  readonly identityId?: string;
    38→};
    39→
    40→/** Dependencies for integration tool execution (extends base with credential resolver). */
    41→export type IntegrationExecutorDeps = ToolExecutorDeps & {
    42→  readonly toolEncryptionKey: string;
    43→};
    44→
    45→/** Anthropic tool_result content block for follow-up requests. */
    46→export type ToolResultMessage = {
    47→  role: "user";
    48→  content: Array<{
    49→    type: "tool_result";
    50→    tool_use_id: string;
    51→    content: string;
    52→    is_error?: boolean;
    53→  }>;
    54→};
    55→
    56→/** Handler for a single Brain-native tool. */
    57→type BrainToolHandler = (
    58→  input: Record<string, unknown>,
    59→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
    60→) => Promise<ToolExecutionResult>;
    61→
    62→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    63→// Brain-Native Tool Handlers
    64→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    65→
    66→const searchEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
    67→  const query = typeof input.query === "string" ? input.query : "";
    68→
    69→  if (!query.trim()) {
    70→    return {
    71→      toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
    72→      content: JSON.stringify({ results: [], message: "Empty search query" }),
    73→      isError: false,
    74→    };
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", deps.workspaceId);
    78→
    79→  // Simple keyword search across tasks, decisions, and features
    80→  // Uses basic string matching (CONTAINS) for the walking skeleton.
    81→  // Future: BM25 fulltext search.
    82→  type EntityRow = {
    83→    id: RecordId;
    84→    table: string;
    85→    title?: string;
    86→    summary?: string;
    87→    text?: string;
    88→    status?: string;
    89→  };
    90→
    91→  const results = await deps.surreal.query<[EntityRow[], EntityRow[], EntityRow[]]>(
    92→    `SELECT id, 'task' AS table, title, status FROM task WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
    93→     SELECT id, 'decision' AS table, summary, status FROM decision WHERE workspace = $ws AND (summary CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
    94→     SELECT id, 'feature' AS table, title, status FROM feature WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;`,
    95→    { ws: workspaceRecord, q: query },
    96→  );
    97→
    98→  const entities = [
    99→    ...(results[0] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   100→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   101→      type: "task",
   102→      title: row.title ?? "",
   103→      status: row.status ?? "",
   104→    })),
   105→    ...(results[1] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   106→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   107→      type: "decision",
   108→      title: row.summary ?? "",
   109→      status: row.status ?? "",
   110→    })),
   111→    ...(results[2] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   112→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   113→      type: "feature",
   114→      title: row.title ?? "",
   115→      status: row.status ?? "",
   116→    })),
   117→  ];
   118→
   119→  return {
   120→    toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
   121→    content: JSON.stringify({ results: entities, count: entities.length }),
   122→    isError: false,
   123→  };
   124→};
   125→
   126→/** Registry of Brain-native tool handlers, keyed by tool name. */
   127→const brainToolHandlers: ReadonlyMap<string, BrainToolHandler> = new Map([
   128→  ["search_entities", searchEntitiesHandler],
   129→]);
   130→
   131→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   132→// Effect Boundary: executeBrainNativeTools
   133→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   134→
   135→/**
   136→ * Execute all brain-native tool calls and return tool_result messages.
   137→ *
   138→ * - Each classified brain-native call is dispatched to its handler.
   139→ * - Unknown handlers produce an error result (graceful, not HTTP 500).
   140→ * - All errors are caught and returned as is_error tool_results.
   141→ */
   142→export async function executeBrainNativeTools(
   143→  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],
   144→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
   145→): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {
   146→  const brainNativeCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(
   147→    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: "brain-native" }> =>
   148→      c.classification === "brain-native",
   149→  );
   150→
   151→  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];
   152→
   153→  for (const call of brainNativeCalls) {
   154→    const handler = brainToolHandlers.get(call.toolUse.name);
   155→
   156→    if (!handler) {
   157→      results.push({
   158→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   159→        content: JSON.stringify({
   160→          error: `No handler registered for brain-native tool: ${call.toolUse.name}`,
   161→        }),
   162→        isError: true,
   163→      });
   164→      continue;
   165→    }
   166→
   167→    const startMs = performance.now();
   168→    try {
   169→      const result = await handler(call.toolUse.input, deps);
   170→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   171→      results.push({
   172→        ...result,
   173→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   174→      });
   175→
   176→      // Fire-and-forget trace capture (do not block tool result delivery)
   177→      captureToolTrace(
   178→        {
   179→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   180→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   181→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   182→          outcome: "success",
   183→          durationMs,
   184→          input: call.toolUse.input as Record<string, unknown>,
   185→          output: { content: result.content },
   186→        },
   187→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   188→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   189→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   190→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   191→          error: String(traceError),
   192→        });
   193→      });
   194→    } catch (error) {
   195→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   196→      log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.handler_error", "Brain-native tool execution failed", {
   197→        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   198→        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,
   199→        error: String(error),
   200→      });
   201→      results.push({
   202→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   203→        content: JSON.stringify({
   204→          error: `Tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,
   205→        }),
   206→        isError: true,
   207→      });
   208→
   209→      // Trace error outcome too
   210→      captureToolTrace(
   211→        {
   212→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   213→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   214→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   215→          outcome: "error",
   216→          durationMs,
   217→        },
   218→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   219→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   220→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   221→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   222→          error: String(traceError),
   223→        });
   224→      });
   225→    }
   226→  }
   227→
   228→  return results;
   229→}
   230→
   231→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   232→// Helper: buildToolResultMessages
   233→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   234→
   235→/**
   236→ * Build an Anthropic tool_result message from execution results.
   237→ * Used to construct the follow-up request in the tool use loop.
   238→ */
   239→export function buildToolResultMessage(
   240→  results: ToolExecutionResult[],
   241→): ToolResultMessage {
   242→  return {
   243→    role: "user",
   244→    content: results.map((result) => ({
   245→      type: "tool_result" as const,
   246→      tool_use_id: result.toolUseId,
   247→      content: result.content,
   248→      ...(result.isError ? { is_error: true } : {}),
   249→    })),
   250→  };
   251→}
   252→
   253→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   254→// Response Sanitization (Pure Functions)
   255→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   256→
   257→/** Headers that must be stripped from integration HTTP responses. */
   258→const SENSITIVE_HEADERS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
   259→  "authorization",
   260→  "set-cookie",
   261→  "x-api-key",
   262→  "www-authenticate",
   263→]);
   264→
   265→/** JSON field names (lowercase) that must be recursively stripped from response bodies. */
   266→const SENSITIVE_FIELDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
   267→  "access_token",
   268→  "refresh_token",
   269→  "api_key",
   270→  "password",
   271→  "client_secret",
   272→  "bearer_token",
   273→]);
   274→
   275→/** Maximum response body size in bytes before truncation. */
   276→const MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 100 * 1024; // 100KB
   277→
   278→/**
   279→ * Strip sensitive headers from an HTTP response headers map.
   280→ * Pure function: returns a new record with sensitive headers removed.
   281→ */
   282→export function sanitizeResponseHeaders(
   283→  headers: Record<string, string>,
   284→): Record<string, string> {
   285→  const sanitized: Record<string, string> = {};
   286→  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(headers)) {
   287→    if (!SENSITIVE_HEADERS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
   288→      sanitized[key] = value;
   289→    }
   290→  }
   291→  return sanitized;
   292→}
   293→
   294→/**
   295→ * Recursively strip sensitive fields from a JSON value.
   296→ * Pure function: returns a new value with credential fields removed.
   297→ */
   298→export function sanitizeResponseBody(value: unknown): unknown {
   299→  if (value === null || value === undefined) return value;
   300→
   301→  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   302→    return value.map(sanitizeResponseBody);
   303→  }
   304→
   305→  if (typeof value === "object") {
   306→    const sanitized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   307→    for (const [key, fieldValue] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
   308→      if (!SENSITIVE_FIELDS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
   309→        sanitized[key] = sanitizeResponseBody(fieldValue);
   310→      }
   311→    }
   312→    return sanitized;
   313→  }
   314→
   315→  return value;
   316→}
   317→
   318→/**
   319→ * Truncate a string to the maximum allowed byte size.
   320→ * Pure function: appends a truncation marker if the content exceeds the limit.
   321→ */
   322→export function truncateResponse(content: string): string {
   323→  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
   324→  const bytes = encoder.encode(content);
   325→  if (bytes.length <= MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) return content;
   326→
   327→  const truncated = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(0, MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES));
   328→  return `${truncated}\n\n[Response truncated: ${bytes.length} bytes exceeds ${MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES} byte limit]`;
   329→}
   330→
   331→/**
   332→ * Full sanitization pipeline for integration tool responses.
   333→ * Pure function: sanitize body -> truncate -> return content string.
   334→ */
   335→export function sanitizeIntegrationResponse(
   336→  responseBody: string,
   337→  responseHeaders: Record<string, string>,
   338→): { content: string; sanitizedHeaders: Record<string, string> } {
   339→  const sanitizedHeaders = sanitizeResponseHeaders(responseHeaders);
   340→
   341→  // Try to parse and sanitize JSON body
   342→  let sanitizedContent: string;
   343→  try {
   344→    const parsed = JSON.parse(responseBody);
   345→    const sanitized = sanitizeResponseBody(parsed);
   346→    sanitizedContent = JSON.stringify(sanitized);
   347→  } catch {
   348→    // Not JSON -- use raw body
   349→    sanitizedContent = responseBody;
   350→  }
   351→
   352→  const truncatedContent = truncateResponse(sanitizedContent);
   353→
   354→  return { content: truncatedContent, sanitizedHeaders };
   355→}
   356→
   357→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   358→// Effect Boundary: executeIntegrationTools
   359→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   360→
   361→/**
   362→ * Execute all integration tool calls via HTTP with brokered credentials.
   363→ *
   364→ * For each integration tool call:
   365→ *   1. Resolve credentials via credential-resolver
   366→ *   2. Execute HTTP call to integration API
   367→ *   3. Sanitize response (strip auth headers + credential fields)
   368→ *   4. Truncate to 100KB
   369→ *   5. Return as tool_result
   370→ *   6. Write trace record
   371→ *
   372→ * Errors are returned as is_error tool_results, never thrown as exceptions.
   373→ */
   374→export async function executeIntegrationTools(
   375→  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],
   376→  deps: IntegrationExecutorDeps,
   377→): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {
   378→  const integrationCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(
   379→    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: "integration" }> =>
   380→      c.classification === "integration",
   381→  );
   382→
   383→  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];
   384→
   385→  for (const call of integrationCalls) {
   386→    const startMs = performance.now();
   387→    const providerRef = call.resolvedTool.toolkit;
   388→
   389→    try {
   390→      // Step 1: Resolve credentials
   391→      if (!deps.identityId) {
   392→        results.push({
   393→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   394→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: "Identity required for integration tool calls" }),
   395→          isError: true,
   396→        });
   397→        continue;
   398→      }
   399→
   400→      const credentialResolverDeps: CredentialResolverDeps = {
   401→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   402→        toolEncryptionKey: deps.toolEncryptionKey,
   403→      };
   404→
   405→      const credResult = await resolveCredentialsForTool(
   406→        call.toolUse.name,
   407→        deps.identityId,
   408→        credentialResolverDeps,
   409→      );
   410→
   411→      if (!credResult.ok) {
   412→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   413→        results.push({
   414→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   415→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: `Credential resolution failed: ${credResult.error}` }),
   416→          isError: true,
   417→        });
   418→
   419→        captureToolTrace(
   420→          {
   421→            toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   422→            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   423→            identityId: deps.identityId,
   424→            outcome: "error",
   425→            durationMs,
   426→            input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   427→          },
   428→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   429→        ).catch((traceError) => {
   430→          log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   431→            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   432→            error: String(traceError),
   433→          });
   434→        });
   435→        continue;
   436→      }
   437→
   438→      // Step 2: Build and execute HTTP request
   439→      // The tool input should contain the endpoint URL or we derive it from provider config
   440→      const endpointUrl = typeof call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url === "string"
   441→        ? call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url
   442→        : typeof call.toolUse.input.url === "string"
   443→          ? call.toolUse.input.url
   444→          : undefined;
   445→
   446→      if (!endpointUrl) {
   447→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   448→        results.push({
   449→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   450→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: "Integration tool call missing endpoint_url or url in input" }),
   451→          isError: true,
   452→        });
   453→
   454→        captureToolTrace(
   455→          {
   456→            toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   457→            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   458→            identityId: deps.identityId,
   459→            outcome: "error",
   460→            durationMs,
   461→            input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   462→          },
   463→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   464→        ).catch((traceError) => {
   465→          log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   466→            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   467→            error: String(traceError),
   468→          });
   469→        });
   470→        continue;
   471→      }
   472→
   473→      const httpMethod = typeof call.toolUse.input.method === "string"
   474→        ? call.toolUse.input.method.toUpperCase()
   475→        : "POST";
   476→
   477→      const requestHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
   478→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   479→        ...credResult.headers,
   480→      };
   481→
   482→      // Build request body from tool input (excluding meta fields)
   483→      const { endpoint_url: _eu, url: _u, method: _m, ...bodyInput } = call.toolUse.input;
   484→      const hasBody = httpMethod !== "GET" && httpMethod !== "HEAD" && Object.keys(bodyInput).length > 0;
   485→
   486→      const fetchOptions: RequestInit = {
   487→        method: httpMethod,
   488→        headers: requestHeaders,
   489→        ...(hasBody ? { body: JSON.stringify(bodyInput) } : {}),
   490→      };
   491→
   492→      const httpResponse = await fetch(endpointUrl, fetchOptions);
   493→      const responseBody = await httpResponse.text();
   494→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   495→
   496→      // Step 3-4: Sanitize and truncate
   497→      const responseHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
   498→      httpResponse.headers.forEach((value, key) => {
   499→        responseHeaders[key] = value;
   500→      });
   501→
   502→      const { content } = sanitizeIntegrationResponse(responseBody, responseHeaders);
   503→
   504→      // Step 5: Return as tool_result
   505→      const isError = httpResponse.status >= 400;
   506→      const resultContent = isError
   507→        ? JSON.stringify({
   508→            error: `Integration API returned ${httpResponse.status}`,
   509→            status: httpResponse.status,
   510→            body: content,
   511→          })
   512→        : content;
   513→
   514→      results.push({
   515→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   516→        content: resultContent,
   517→        isError,
   518→      });
   519→
   520→      // Step 6: Write trace record
   521→      captureToolTrace(
   522→        {
   523→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   524→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   525→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   526→          outcome: isError ? "error" : "success",
   527→          durationMs,
   528→          input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   529→          output: { status: httpResponse.status, content_length: content.length },
   530→        },
   531→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   532→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   533→        log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   534→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   535→          error: String(traceError),
   536→        });
   537→      });
   538→    } catch (error) {
   539→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   540→
   541→      // Integration errors are tool_result errors, not HTTP 500s
   542→      log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.error", "Integration tool execution failed", {
   543→        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   544→        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,
   545→        error: String(error),
   546→      });
   547→
   548→      results.push({
   549→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   550→        content: JSON.stringify({
   551→          error: `Integration tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,
   552→        }),
   553→        isError: true,
   554→      });
   555→
   556→      captureToolTrace(
   557→        {
   558→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   559→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   560→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   561→          outcome: "error",
   562→          durationMs,
   563→          input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   564→        },
   565→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   566→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   567→        log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   568→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   569→          error: String(traceError),
   570→        });
   571→      });
   572→    }
   573→  }
   574→
   575→  return results;
   576→}
   577→

     1→/**
     2→ * Tool Executor -- Effect Boundary
     3→ *
     4→ * Executes Brain-native tool calls via graph queries. This is the adapter/effect
     5→ * boundary -- it performs IO (SurrealDB queries) to fulfill tool calls that the
     6→ * pure tool-router classified as "brain-native".
     7→ *
     8→ * Each Brain-native tool handler is a function: (input, deps) -> ToolExecutionResult.
     9→ * Unknown tool names produce an error result (not an exception).
    10→ *
    11→ * Step 8.5 in the proxy pipeline.
    12→ */
    13→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    14→import type { ClassifiedToolCall } from "./tool-router";
    15→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";
    16→import { captureToolTrace } from "./tool-trace-writer";
    17→import {
    18→  resolveCredentialsForTool,
    19→  type CredentialResolverDeps,
    20→} from "./credential-resolver";
    21→import {
    22→  fetchGovernancePolicies,
    23→  countTodayToolExecutions,
    24→  fetchCanUseRateLimit,
    25→  countHourlyToolExecutions,
    26→  type GovernsPolicyRow,
    27→} from "../tool-registry/queries";
    28→
    29→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→// Types
    31→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→/** Result of executing a single Brain-native tool call. */
    34→export type ToolExecutionResult = {
    35→  readonly toolUseId: string;
    36→  readonly content: string;
    37→  readonly isError: boolean;
    38→};
    39→
    40→/** Dependencies injected into tool executors. */
    41→export type ToolExecutorDeps = {
    42→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    43→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    44→  readonly identityId?: string;
    45→};
    46→
    47→/** Dependencies for integration tool execution (extends base with credential resolver). */
    48→export type IntegrationExecutorDeps = ToolExecutorDeps & {
    49→  readonly toolEncryptionKey: string;
    50→};
    51→
    52→/** Anthropic tool_result content block for follow-up requests. */
    53→export type ToolResultMessage = {
    54→  role: "user";
    55→  content: Array<{
    56→    type: "tool_result";
    57→    tool_use_id: string;
    58→    content: string;
    59→    is_error?: boolean;
    60→  }>;
    61→};
    62→
    63→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→// Governance Types (Pure)
    65→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    66→
    67→/** Result of evaluating governance policies for a tool call. */
    68→export type GovernanceVerdict =
    69→  | { readonly allowed: true }
    70→  | { readonly allowed: false; readonly reason: string; readonly outcome: "denied" | "rate_limited" };
    71→
    72→/**
    73→ * Evaluate governance policies (pure function).
    74→ *
    75→ * Rules:
    76→ *   - "requires_human_approval" condition -> denied
    77→ *   - max_per_day exceeded -> denied
    78→ *   - Multiple policies: most restrictive wins (any denial = denied)
    79→ */
    80→export function evaluateGovernancePolicies(
    81→  policies: GovernsPolicyRow[],
    82→  todayExecutionCount: number,
    83→): GovernanceVerdict {
    84→  for (const policy of policies) {
    85→    // Check requires_human_approval condition
    86→    if (policy.conditions === "requires_human_approval") {
    87→      return {
    88→        allowed: false,
    89→        reason: `Tool call denied by policy "${policy.policyTitle}": requires human approval`,
    90→        outcome: "denied",
    91→      };
    92→    }
    93→
    94→    // Check max_per_day limit
    95→    if (policy.max_per_day !== undefined && todayExecutionCount >= policy.max_per_day) {
    96→      return {
    97→        allowed: false,
    98→        reason: `Tool call denied by policy "${policy.policyTitle}": daily limit of ${policy.max_per_day} exceeded (${todayExecutionCount} calls today)`,
    99→        outcome: "denied",
   100→      };
   101→    }
   102→  }
   103→
   104→  return { allowed: true };
   105→}
   106→
   107→/**
   108→ * Evaluate can_use rate limit (pure function).
   109→ */
   110→export function evaluateRateLimit(
   111→  maxCallsPerHour: number | undefined,
   112→  hourlyExecutionCount: number,
   113→): GovernanceVerdict {
   114→  if (maxCallsPerHour !== undefined && hourlyExecutionCount >= maxCallsPerHour) {
   115→    return {
   116→      allowed: false,
   117→      reason: `Rate limit exceeded: ${hourlyExecutionCount}/${maxCallsPerHour} calls per hour`,
   118→      outcome: "rate_limited",
   119→    };
   120→  }
   121→
   122→  return { allowed: true };
   123→}
   124→
   125→/** Handler for a single Brain-native tool. */
   126→type BrainToolHandler = (
   127→  input: Record<string, unknown>,
   128→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
   129→) => Promise<ToolExecutionResult>;
   130→
   131→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   132→// Brain-Native Tool Handlers
   133→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   134→
   135→const searchEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
   136→  const query = typeof input.query === "string" ? input.query : "";
   137→
   138→  if (!query.trim()) {
   139→    return {
   140→      toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
   141→      content: JSON.stringify({ results: [], message: "Empty search query" }),
   142→      isError: false,
   143→    };
   144→  }
   145→
   146→  const workspaceRecord = new RecordId("workspace", deps.workspaceId);
   147→
   148→  // Simple keyword search across tasks, decisions, and features
   149→  // Uses basic string matching (CONTAINS) for the walking skeleton.
   150→  // Future: BM25 fulltext search.
   151→  type EntityRow = {
   152→    id: RecordId;
   153→    table: string;
   154→    title?: string;
   155→    summary?: string;
   156→    text?: string;
   157→    status?: string;
   158→  };
   159→
   160→  const results = await deps.surreal.query<[EntityRow[], EntityRow[], EntityRow[]]>(
   161→    `SELECT id, 'task' AS table, title, status FROM task WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
   162→     SELECT id, 'decision' AS table, summary, status FROM decision WHERE workspace = $ws AND (summary CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;
   163→     SELECT id, 'feature' AS table, title, status FROM feature WHERE workspace = $ws AND (title CONTAINS $q) LIMIT 10;`,
   164→    { ws: workspaceRecord, q: query },
   165→  );
   166→
   167→  const entities = [
   168→    ...(results[0] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   169→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   170→      type: "task",
   171→      title: row.title ?? "",
   172→      status: row.status ?? "",
   173→    })),
   174→    ...(results[1] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   175→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   176→      type: "decision",
   177→      title: row.summary ?? "",
   178→      status: row.status ?? "",
   179→    })),
   180→    ...(results[2] ?? []).map((row) => ({
   181→      id: (row.id as RecordId).id as string,
   182→      type: "feature",
   183→      title: row.title ?? "",
   184→      status: row.status ?? "",
   185→    })),
   186→  ];
   187→
   188→  return {
   189→    toolUseId: "", // filled by caller
   190→    content: JSON.stringify({ results: entities, count: entities.length }),
   191→    isError: false,
   192→  };
   193→};
   194→
   195→/** Registry of Brain-native tool handlers, keyed by tool name. */
   196→const brainToolHandlers: ReadonlyMap<string, BrainToolHandler> = new Map([
   197→  ["search_entities", searchEntitiesHandler],
   198→]);
   199→
   200→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   201→// Effect Boundary: executeBrainNativeTools
   202→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   203→
   204→/**
   205→ * Execute all brain-native tool calls and return tool_result messages.
   206→ *
   207→ * - Each classified brain-native call is dispatched to its handler.
   208→ * - Unknown handlers produce an error result (graceful, not HTTP 500).
   209→ * - All errors are caught and returned as is_error tool_results.
   210→ */
   211→export async function executeBrainNativeTools(
   212→  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],
   213→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
   214→): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {
   215→  const brainNativeCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(
   216→    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: "brain-native" }> =>
   217→      c.classification === "brain-native",
   218→  );
   219→
   220→  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];
   221→
   222→  for (const call of brainNativeCalls) {
   223→    const handler = brainToolHandlers.get(call.toolUse.name);
   224→
   225→    if (!handler) {
   226→      results.push({
   227→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   228→        content: JSON.stringify({
   229→          error: `No handler registered for brain-native tool: ${call.toolUse.name}`,
   230→        }),
   231→        isError: true,
   232→      });
   233→      continue;
   234→    }
   235→
   236→    const startMs = performance.now();
   237→    try {
   238→      const result = await handler(call.toolUse.input, deps);
   239→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   240→      results.push({
   241→        ...result,
   242→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   243→      });
   244→
   245→      // Fire-and-forget trace capture (do not block tool result delivery)
   246→      captureToolTrace(
   247→        {
   248→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   249→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   250→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   251→          outcome: "success",
   252→          durationMs,
   253→          input: call.toolUse.input as Record<string, unknown>,
   254→          output: { content: result.content },
   255→        },
   256→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   257→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   258→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   259→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   260→          error: String(traceError),
   261→        });
   262→      });
   263→    } catch (error) {
   264→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   265→      log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.handler_error", "Brain-native tool execution failed", {
   266→        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   267→        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,
   268→        error: String(error),
   269→      });
   270→      results.push({
   271→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   272→        content: JSON.stringify({
   273→          error: `Tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,
   274→        }),
   275→        isError: true,
   276→      });
   277→
   278→      // Trace error outcome too
   279→      captureToolTrace(
   280→        {
   281→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   282→          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   283→          identityId: deps.identityId,
   284→          outcome: "error",
   285→          durationMs,
   286→        },
   287→        { surreal: deps.surreal },
   288→      ).catch((traceError) => {
   289→        log.warn("proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed", "Tool trace capture failed", {
   290→          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   291→          error: String(traceError),
   292→        });
   293→      });
   294→    }
   295→  }
   296→
   297→  return results;
   298→}
   299→
   300→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   301→// Helper: buildToolResultMessages
   302→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   303→
   304→/**
   305→ * Build an Anthropic tool_result message from execution results.
   306→ * Used to construct the follow-up request in the tool use loop.
   307→ */
   308→export function buildToolResultMessage(
   309→  results: ToolExecutionResult[],
   310→): ToolResultMessage {
   311→  return {
   312→    role: "user",
   313→    content: results.map((result) => ({
   314→      type: "tool_result" as const,
   315→      tool_use_id: result.toolUseId,
   316→      content: result.content,
   317→      ...(result.isError ? { is_error: true } : {}),
   318→    })),
   319→  };
   320→}
   321→
   322→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   323→// Response Sanitization (Pure Functions)
   324→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   325→
   326→/** Headers that must be stripped from integration HTTP responses. */
   327→const SENSITIVE_HEADERS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
   328→  "authorization",
   329→  "set-cookie",
   330→  "x-api-key",
   331→  "www-authenticate",
   332→]);
   333→
   334→/** JSON field names (lowercase) that must be recursively stripped from response bodies. */
   335→const SENSITIVE_FIELDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
   336→  "access_token",
   337→  "refresh_token",
   338→  "api_key",
   339→  "password",
   340→  "client_secret",
   341→  "bearer_token",
   342→]);
   343→
   344→/** Maximum response body size in bytes before truncation. */
   345→const MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 100 * 1024; // 100KB
   346→
   347→/**
   348→ * Strip sensitive headers from an HTTP response headers map.
   349→ * Pure function: returns a new record with sensitive headers removed.
   350→ */
   351→export function sanitizeResponseHeaders(
   352→  headers: Record<string, string>,
   353→): Record<string, string> {
   354→  const sanitized: Record<string, string> = {};
   355→  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(headers)) {
   356→    if (!SENSITIVE_HEADERS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
   357→      sanitized[key] = value;
   358→    }
   359→  }
   360→  return sanitized;
   361→}
   362→
   363→/**
   364→ * Recursively strip sensitive fields from a JSON value.
   365→ * Pure function: returns a new value with credential fields removed.
   366→ */
   367→export function sanitizeResponseBody(value: unknown): unknown {
   368→  if (value === null || value === undefined) return value;
   369→
   370→  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   371→    return value.map(sanitizeResponseBody);
   372→  }
   373→
   374→  if (typeof value === "object") {
   375→    const sanitized: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   376→    for (const [key, fieldValue] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
   377→      if (!SENSITIVE_FIELDS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {
   378→        sanitized[key] = sanitizeResponseBody(fieldValue);
   379→      }
   380→    }
   381→    return sanitized;
   382→  }
   383→
   384→  return value;
   385→}
   386→
   387→/**
   388→ * Truncate a string to the maximum allowed byte size.
   389→ * Pure function: appends a truncation marker if the content exceeds the limit.
   390→ */
   391→export function truncateResponse(content: string): string {
   392→  const encoder = new TextEncoder();
   393→  const bytes = encoder.encode(content);
   394→  if (bytes.length <= MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) return content;
   395→
   396→  const truncated = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(0, MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES));
   397→  return `${truncated}\n\n[Response truncated: ${bytes.length} bytes exceeds ${MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES} byte limit]`;
   398→}
   399→
   400→/**
   401→ * Full sanitization pipeline for integration tool responses.
   402→ * Pure function: sanitize body -> truncate -> return content string.
   403→ */
   404→export function sanitizeIntegrationResponse(
   405→  responseBody: string,
   406→  responseHeaders: Record<string, string>,
   407→): { content: string; sanitizedHeaders: Record<string, string> } {
   408→  const sanitizedHeaders = sanitizeResponseHeaders(responseHeaders);
   409→
   410→  // Try to parse and sanitize JSON body
   411→  let sanitizedContent: string;
   412→  try {
   413→    const parsed = JSON.parse(responseBody);
   414→    const sanitized = sanitizeResponseBody(parsed);
   415→    sanitizedContent = JSON.stringify(sanitized);
   416→  } catch {
   417→    // Not JSON -- use raw body
   418→    sanitizedContent = responseBody;
   419→  }
   420→
   421→  const truncatedContent = truncateResponse(sanitizedContent);
   422→
   423→  return { content: truncatedContent, sanitizedHeaders };
   424→}
   425→
   426→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   427→// Effect Boundary: executeIntegrationTools
   428→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   429→
   430→/**
   431→ * Execute all integration tool calls via HTTP with brokered credentials.
   432→ *
   433→ * For each integration tool call:
   434→ *   1. Resolve credentials via credential-resolver
   435→ *   2. Execute HTTP call to integration API
   436→ *   3. Sanitize response (strip auth headers + credential fields)
   437→ *   4. Truncate to 100KB
   438→ *   5. Return as tool_result
   439→ *   6. Write trace record
   440→ *
   441→ * Errors are returned as is_error tool_results, never thrown as exceptions.
   442→ */
   443→export async function executeIntegrationTools(
   444→  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],
   445→  deps: IntegrationExecutorDeps,
   446→): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {
   447→  const integrationCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(
   448→    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: "integration" }> =>
   449→      c.classification === "integration",
   450→  );
   451→
   452→  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];
   453→
   454→  for (const call of integrationCalls) {
   455→    const startMs = performance.now();
   456→    const providerRef = call.resolvedTool.toolkit;
   457→
   458→    try {
   459→      // Step 0: Governance check (BEFORE credential resolution)
   460→      const governancePolicies = await fetchGovernancePolicies(
   461→        deps.surreal,
   462→        call.toolUse.name,
   463→      );
   464→
   465→      if (governancePolicies.length > 0) {
   466→        const todayCount = await countTodayToolExecutions(
   467→          deps.surreal,
   468→          call.toolUse.name,
   469→          deps.workspaceId,
   470→        );
   471→
   472→        const governanceVerdict = evaluateGovernancePolicies(governancePolicies, todayCount);
   473→
   474→        if (!governanceVerdict.allowed) {
   475→          const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   476→          results.push({
   477→            toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   478→            content: JSON.stringify({ error: governanceVerdict.reason }),
   479→            isError: true,
   480→          });
   481→
   482→          captureToolTrace(
   483→            {
   484→              toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   485→              workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   486→              identityId: deps.identityId,
   487→              outcome: governanceVerdict.outcome,
   488→              durationMs,
   489→              input: { tool_kind: "integration", governance_denial: governanceVerdict.reason },
   490→            },
   491→            { surreal: deps.surreal },
   492→          ).catch((traceError) => {
   493→            log.warn("proxy.governance.trace_failed", "Governance trace capture failed", {
   494→              tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   495→              error: String(traceError),
   496→            });
   497→          });
   498→          continue;
   499→        }
   500→      }
   501→
   502→      // Step 0.5: Rate limit check on can_use edge
   503→      if (deps.identityId) {
   504→        const { maxCallsPerHour } = await fetchCanUseRateLimit(
   505→          deps.surreal,
   506→          deps.identityId,
   507→          call.toolUse.name,
   508→        );
   509→
   510→        if (maxCallsPerHour !== undefined) {
   511→          const hourlyCount = await countHourlyToolExecutions(
   512→            deps.surreal,
   513→            call.toolUse.name,
   514→            deps.workspaceId,
   515→            deps.identityId,
   516→          );
   517→
   518→          const rateLimitVerdict = evaluateRateLimit(maxCallsPerHour, hourlyCount);
   519→
   520→          if (!rateLimitVerdict.allowed) {
   521→            const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   522→            results.push({
   523→              toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   524→              content: JSON.stringify({ error: rateLimitVerdict.reason }),
   525→              isError: true,
   526→            });
   527→
   528→            captureToolTrace(
   529→              {
   530→                toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   531→                workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   532→                identityId: deps.identityId,
   533→                outcome: rateLimitVerdict.outcome,
   534→                durationMs,
   535→                input: { tool_kind: "integration", rate_limit_denial: rateLimitVerdict.reason },
   536→              },
   537→              { surreal: deps.surreal },
   538→            ).catch((traceError) => {
   539→              log.warn("proxy.rate_limit.trace_failed", "Rate limit trace capture failed", {
   540→                tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   541→                error: String(traceError),
   542→              });
   543→            });
   544→            continue;
   545→          }
   546→        }
   547→      }
   548→
   549→      // Step 1: Resolve credentials
   550→      if (!deps.identityId) {
   551→        results.push({
   552→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   553→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: "Identity required for integration tool calls" }),
   554→          isError: true,
   555→        });
   556→        continue;
   557→      }
   558→
   559→      const credentialResolverDeps: CredentialResolverDeps = {
   560→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   561→        toolEncryptionKey: deps.toolEncryptionKey,
   562→      };
   563→
   564→      const credResult = await resolveCredentialsForTool(
   565→        call.toolUse.name,
   566→        deps.identityId,
   567→        credentialResolverDeps,
   568→      );
   569→
   570→      if (!credResult.ok) {
   571→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   572→        results.push({
   573→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   574→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: `Credential resolution failed: ${credResult.error}` }),
   575→          isError: true,
   576→        });
   577→
   578→        captureToolTrace(
   579→          {
   580→            toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   581→            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   582→            identityId: deps.identityId,
   583→            outcome: "error",
   584→            durationMs,
   585→            input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   586→          },
   587→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   588→        ).catch((traceError) => {
   589→          log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   590→            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   591→            error: String(traceError),
   592→          });
   593→        });
   594→        continue;
   595→      }
   596→
   597→      // Step 2: Build and execute HTTP request
   598→      // The tool input should contain the endpoint URL or we derive it from provider config
   599→      const endpointUrl = typeof call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url === "string"
   600→        ? call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url
   601→        : typeof call.toolUse.input.url === "string"
   602→          ? call.toolUse.input.url
   603→          : undefined;
   604→
   605→      if (!endpointUrl) {
   606→        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   607→        results.push({
   608→          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   609→          content: JSON.stringify({ error: "Integration tool call missing endpoint_url or url in input" }),
   610→          isError: true,
   611→        });
   612→
   613→        captureToolTrace(
   614→          {
   615→            toolName: call.toolUse.name,
   616→            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,
   617→            identityId: deps.identityId,
   618→            outcome: "error",
   619→            durationMs,
   620→            input: { tool_kind: "integration", credential_provider: providerRef },
   621→          },
   622→          { surreal: deps.surreal },
   623→        ).catch((traceError) => {
   624→          log.warn("proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed", "Integration trace capture failed", {
   625→            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,
   626→            error: String(traceError),
   627→          });
   628→        });
   629→        continue;
   630→      }
   631→
   632→      const httpMethod = typeof call.toolUse.input.method === "string"
   633→        ? call.toolUse.input.method.toUpperCase()
   634→        : "POST";
   635→
   636→      const requestHeaders: Record<string, string> = {
   637→        "Content-Type": "application/json",
   638→        ...credResult.headers,
   639→      };
   640→
   641→      // Build request body from tool input (excluding meta fields)
   642→      const { endpoint_url: _eu, url: _u, method: _m, ...bodyInput } = call.toolUse.input;
   643→      const hasBody = httpMethod !== "GET" && httpMethod !== "HEAD" && Object.keys(bodyInput).length > 0;
   644→
   645→      const fetchOptions: RequestInit = {
   646→        method: httpMethod,
   647→        headers: requestHeaders,
   648→        ...(hasBody ? { body: JSON.stringify(bodyInput) } : {}),
   649→      };
   650→
   651→      const httpResponse = await fetch(endpointUrl, fetchOptions);
   652→      const responseBody = await httpResponse.text();
   653→      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;
   654→
   655→      // Step 3-4: Sanitize and truncate
   656→      const responseHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};
   657→      httpResponse.headers.forEach((value, key) => {
   658→        responseHeaders[key] = value;
   659→      });
   660→
   661→      const { content } = sanitizeIntegrationResponse(responseBody, responseHeaders);
   662→
   663→      // Step 5: Return as tool_result
   664→      const isError = httpResponse.status >= 400;
   665→      const resultContent = isError
   666→        ? JSON.stringify({
   667→            error: `Integration API returned ${httpResponse.status}`,
   668→            status: httpResponse.status,
   669→            body: content,
   670→          })
   671→        : content;
   672→
   673→      results.push({
   674→        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,
   675→        content: resultContent,
   676→        isError,
   677→      });
   678→
   679→      // Step 6: Write trace record
   680→      captureToolTrace(
   681→        {
   682→          toolName: call.toolUse.name,

BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {\n  const proposedAction = typeof input.proposed_action === \"string\" ? input.proposed_action : \"\";\n  if (!proposedAction.trim()) return [\"decision\"],\n    limit: 14,\n  });\n\n  const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(proposedAction);\n  const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n  const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n  const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n\n  for softTensions.push({\n        id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`, name: candidate.name,\n        score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n        reason: overlap\n          ? \"Related soft_tensions: softTensions, supporting, proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0 });\n};\n\n/** Registry of Brain-native tool handlers, keyed by tool name. */\nconst brainToolHandlers: ReadonlyMap<string, executeBrainNativeTools(\n  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],\n  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,\n): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {\n  const brainNativeCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(\n    (c): c is toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n      });\n\n      // Fire-and-forget trace capture (do not block tool result delivery)\n      captureToolTrace(\n        {\n          toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n      output: { content: result.content },\n        },\n        { surreal: deps.surreal },\n      ).catch((traceError) => {\n        log.warn(\"proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed\", \"Tool trace capture log.warn(\"proxy.tool_executor.handler_error\", \"Brain-native tool execution failed\", {\n        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,\n        error: String(error),\n log.warn(\"proxy.tool_executor.trace_failed\", \"Tool trace capture failed\", {\n          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n          error: String(traceError),\n        });\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return results;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Helper: buildToolResultMessages\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Build an Anthropic tool_result message from execution results.\n * Used to construct the follow-up request in the tool use loop.\n */\nexport function buildToolResultMessage(\n  results: ToolExecutionResult[],\n): ToolResultMessage {\n  return {\n    role: \"user\",\n    content: results.map((result) => ({\n      type: \"tool_result\" as const,\n      tool_use_id: result.toolUseId,\n      content: result.content,\n      ...(result.isError ? { is_error: true } : {}),\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Response Sanitization (Pure Functions)\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/** Headers that must be stripped from integration HTTP responses. */\nconst SENSITIVE_HEADERS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n  \"authorization\",\n  \"set-cookie\",\n  \"x-api-key\",\n  \"www-authenticate\",\n]);\n\n/** JSON field names (lowercase) that must be recursively stripped from response bodies. */\nconst SENSITIVE_FIELDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n  \"access_token\",\n  \"refresh_token\",\n  \"api_key\",\n  \"password\",\n  \"client_secret\",\n  \"bearer_token\",\n]);\n\n/** Maximum response body size in bytes before truncation. */\nconst MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 100 * 1024; // 100KB\n\n/**\n * Strip sensitive headers from an HTTP response headers map.\n * Pure function: returns a new record with sensitive headers removed.\n */\nexport function sanitizeResponseHeaders(\n  headers: Record<string, string>,\n): Record<string, string> {\n  const sanitized: Record<string, string> = {};\n  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(headers)) {\n    if (!SENSITIVE_HEADERS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {\n      sanitized[key] = value;\n    }\n  }\n  return sanitized;\n}\n\n/**\n * Recursively strip sensitive fields from a JSON value.\n * Pure function: returns a new value with credential fields removed.\n */\nexport function sanitizeResponseBody(value: unknown): unknown {\n  if (value === null || value === undefined) return value;\n\n  if (Array.isArray(value)) {\n    return value.map(sanitizeResponseBody);\n  }\n\n  if (typeof value === \"object\") {\n    const sanitized: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n    for (const [key, fieldValue] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {\n      if (!SENSITIVE_FIELDS.has(key.toLowerCase())) {\n        sanitized[key] = sanitizeResponseBody(fieldValue);\n      }\n    }\n    return sanitized;\n  }\n\n  return value;\n}\n\n/**\n * Truncate a string to the maximum allowed byte size.\n * Pure function: appends a truncation marker if the content exceeds the limit.\n */\nexport function truncateResponse(content: string): string {\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  const bytes = encoder.encode(content);\n  if (bytes.length <= MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) return content;\n\n  const truncated = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.slice(0, MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES));\n  return `${truncated}\\n\\n[Response truncated: ${bytes.length} bytes exceeds ${MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES} byte limit]`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Full sanitization pipeline for integration tool responses.\n * Pure function: sanitize body -> truncate -> return content string.\n */\nexport function sanitizeIntegrationResponse(\n  responseBody: string,\n  responseHeaders: Record<string, string>,\n): { content: string; sanitizedHeaders: Record<string, string> } {\n  const sanitizedHeaders = sanitizeResponseHeaders(responseHeaders);\n\n  // Try to parse and sanitize JSON body\n  let sanitizedContent: string;\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(responseBody);\n    const sanitized = sanitizeResponseBody(parsed);\n    sanitizedContent = JSON.stringify(sanitized);\n  } catch {\n    // Not JSON -- use raw body\n    sanitizedContent = responseBody;\n  }\n\n  const truncatedContent = truncateResponse(sanitizedContent);\n\n  return { content: truncatedContent, sanitizedHeaders };\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Effect Boundary: executeIntegrationTools\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/**\n * Execute all integration tool calls via HTTP with brokered credentials.\n *\n * For each integration tool call:\n *   1. Resolve credentials via credential-resolver\n *   2. Execute HTTP call to integration API\n *   3. Sanitize response (strip auth headers + credential fields)\n *   4. Truncate to 100KB\n *   5. Return as tool_result\n *   6. Write trace record\n *\n * Errors are returned as is_error tool_results, never thrown as exceptions.\n */\nexport async function executeIntegrationTools(\n  classifiedCalls: ClassifiedToolCall[],\n  deps: IntegrationExecutorDeps,\n): Promise<ToolExecutionResult[]> {\n  const integrationCalls = classifiedCalls.filter(\n    (c): c is Extract<ClassifiedToolCall, { classification: \"integration\" }> =>\n      c.classification === \"integration\",\n  );\n\n  const results: ToolExecutionResult[] = [];\n\n  for (const call of integrationCalls) {\n    const startMs = performance.now();\n    const providerRef = call.resolvedTool.toolkit;\n\n    try {\n      // Step 0: Governance check (BEFORE credential resolution)\n      const governancePolicies = await fetchGovernancePolicies(\n        deps.surreal,\n        call.toolUse.name,\n      );\n\n      if (governancePolicies.length > 0) {\n        const todayCount = await countTodayToolExecutions(\n          deps.surreal,\n          call.toolUse.name,\n          deps.workspaceId,\n        );\n\n        const governanceVerdict = evaluateGovernancePolicies(governancePolicies, todayCount);\n\n        if (!governanceVerdict.allowed) {\n          const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;\n          results.push({\n            toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n            content: JSON.stringify({ error: governanceVerdict.reason }),\n            isError: true,\n          });\n\n          captureToolTrace(\n            {\n              toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n              workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n              identityId: deps.identityId,\n              outcome: governanceVerdict.outcome,\n              durationMs,\n              input: { tool_kind: \"integration\", governance_denial: governanceVerdict.reason },\n            },\n            { surreal: deps.surreal },\n          ).catch((traceError) => {\n            log.warn(\"proxy.governance.trace_failed\", \"Governance trace capture failed\", {\n              tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n              error: String(traceError),\n            });\n          });\n          continue;\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Step 0.5: Rate limit check on can_use edge\n      if (deps.identityId) {\n        const { maxCallsPerHour } = await fetchCanUseRateLimit(\n          deps.surreal,\n          deps.identityId,\n          call.toolUse.name,\n        );\n\n        if (maxCallsPerHour !== undefined) {\n          const hourlyCount = await countHourlyToolExecutions(\n            deps.surreal,\n            call.toolUse.name,\n            deps.workspaceId,\n            deps.identityId,\n          );\n\n          const rateLimitVerdict = evaluateRateLimit(maxCallsPerHour, hourlyCount);\n\n          if (!rateLimitVerdict.allowed) {\n            const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;\n            results.push({\n              toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n              content: JSON.stringify({ error: rateLimitVerdict.reason }),\n              isError: true,\n            });\n\n            captureToolTrace(\n              {\n                toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n                workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n                identityId: deps.identityId,\n                outcome: rateLimitVerdict.outcome,\n                durationMs,\n                input: { tool_kind: \"integration\", rate_limit_denial: rateLimitVerdict.reason },\n              },\n              { surreal: deps.surreal },\n            ).catch((traceError) => {\n              log.warn(\"proxy.rate_limit.trace_failed\", \"Rate limit trace capture failed\", {\n                tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n                error: String(traceError),\n              });\n            });\n            continue;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      // Step 1: Resolve credentials\n      if (!deps.identityId) {\n        results.push({\n          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n          content: JSON.stringify({ error: \"Identity required for integration tool calls\" }),\n          isError: true,\n        });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const credentialResolverDeps: CredentialResolverDeps = {\n        surreal: deps.surreal,\n        toolEncryptionKey: deps.toolEncryptionKey,\n      };\n\n      const credResult = await resolveCredentialsForTool(\n        call.toolUse.name,\n        deps.identityId,\n        credentialResolverDeps,\n      );\n\n      if (!credResult.ok) {\n        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;\n        results.push({\n          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n          content: JSON.stringify({ error: `Credential resolution failed: ${credResult.error}` }),\n          isError: true,\n        });\n\n        captureToolTrace(\n          {\n            toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n            identityId: deps.identityId,\n            outcome: \"error\",\n            durationMs,\n            input: { tool_kind: \"integration\", credential_provider: providerRef },\n          },\n          { surreal: deps.surreal },\n        ).catch((traceError) => {\n          log.warn(\"proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed\", \"Integration trace capture failed\", {\n            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n            error: String(traceError),\n          });\n        });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      // Step 2: Build and execute HTTP request\n      // The tool input should contain the endpoint URL or we derive it from provider config\n      const endpointUrl = typeof call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url === \"string\"\n        ? call.toolUse.input.endpoint_url\n        : typeof call.toolUse.input.url === \"string\"\n          ? call.toolUse.input.url\n          : undefined;\n\n      if (!endpointUrl) {\n        const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;\n        results.push({\n          toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n          content: JSON.stringify({ error: \"Integration tool call missing endpoint_url or url in input\" }),\n          isError: true,\n        });\n\n        captureToolTrace(\n          {\n            toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n            workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n            identityId: deps.identityId,\n            outcome: \"error\",\n            durationMs,\n            input: { tool_kind: \"integration\", credential_provider: providerRef },\n          },\n          { surreal: deps.surreal },\n        ).catch((traceError) => {\n          log.warn(\"proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed\", \"Integration trace capture failed\", {\n            tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n            error: String(traceError),\n          });\n        });\n        continue;\n      }\n\n      const httpMethod = typeof call.toolUse.input.method === \"string\"\n        ? call.toolUse.input.method.toUpperCase()\n        : \"POST\";\n\n      const requestHeaders: Record<string, string> = {\n        \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n        ...credResult.headers,\n      };\n\n      // Build request body from tool input (excluding meta fields)\n      const { endpoint_url: _eu, url: _u, method: _m, ...bodyInput } = call.toolUse.input;\n      const hasBody = httpMethod !== \"GET\" && httpMethod !== \"HEAD\" && Object.keys(bodyInput).length > 0;\n\n      const fetchOptions: RequestInit = {\n        method: httpMethod,\n        headers: requestHeaders,\n        ...(hasBody ? { body: JSON.stringify(bodyInput) } : {}),\n      };\n\n      const httpResponse = await fetch(endpointUrl, fetchOptions);\n      const responseBody = await httpResponse.text();\n      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;\n\n      // Step 3-4: Sanitize and truncate\n      const responseHeaders: Record<string, string> = {};\n      httpResponse.headers.forEach((value, key) => {\n        responseHeaders[key] = value;\n      });\n\n      const { content } = sanitizeIntegrationResponse(responseBody, responseHeaders);\n\n      // Step 5: Return as tool_result\n      const isError = httpResponse.status >= 400;\n      const resultContent = isError\n        ? JSON.stringify({\n            error: `Integration API returned ${httpResponse.status}`,\n            status: httpResponse.status,\n            body: content,\n          })\n        : content;\n\n      results.push({\n        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n        content: resultContent,\n        isError,\n      });\n\n      // Step 6: Write trace record\n      captureToolTrace(\n        {\n          toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n          identityId: deps.identityId,\n          outcome: isError ? \"error\" : \"success\",\n          durationMs,\n          input: { tool_kind: \"integration\", credential_provider: providerRef },\n          output: { status: httpResponse.status, content_length: content.length },\n        },\n        { surreal: deps.surreal },\n      ).catch((traceError) => {\n        log.warn(\"proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed\", \"Integration trace capture failed\", {\n          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n          error: String(traceError),\n        });\n      });\n    } catch (error) {\n      const durationMs = performance.now() - startMs;\n\n      // Integration errors are tool_result errors, not HTTP 500s\n      log.warn(\"proxy.integration_executor.error\", \"Integration tool execution failed\", {\n        tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n        tool_use_id: call.toolUse.id,\n        error: String(error),\n      });\n\n      results.push({\n        toolUseId: call.toolUse.id,\n        content: JSON.stringify({\n          error: `Integration tool execution failed: ${String(error)}`,\n        }),\n        isError: true,\n      });\n\n      captureToolTrace(\n        {\n          toolName: call.toolUse.name,\n          workspaceId: deps.workspaceId,\n          identityId: deps.identityId,\n          outcome: \"error\",\n          durationMs,\n          input: { tool_kind: \"integration\", credential_provider: providerRef },\n        },\n        { surreal: deps.surreal },\n      ).catch((traceError) => {\n        log.warn(\"proxy.integration_executor.trace_failed\", \"Integration trace capture failed\", {\n          tool_name: call.toolUse.name,\n          error: String(traceError),\n        });\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  return results;\n}\n"
}
```
