```
     1→/**
     2→ * Tool Router -- Pure Function
     3→ *
     4→ * Classifies tool_use blocks from Anthropic responses as brain-native, integration,
     5→ * or unknown based on the resolved toolset from step 7.5.
     6→ *
     7→ * Pure: no side effects, no IO imports. Takes data in, returns classification out.
     8→ *
     9→ * Step 8.5 in the proxy pipeline (between response read and client return).
    10→ */
    11→import type { ResolvedTool } from "./tool-injector";
    12→
    13→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→// Types
    15→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    16→
    17→/** Anthropic tool_use content block from a Messages API response. */
    18→export type ToolUseBlock = {
    19→  type: "tool_use";
    20→  id: string;
    21→  name: string;
    22→  input: Record<string, unknown>;
    23→};
    24→
    25→/** Classification result for a single tool_use block. */
    26→export type ClassifiedToolCall =
    27→  | { classification: "brain-native"; toolUse: ToolUseBlock; resolvedTool: ResolvedTool }
    28→  | { classification: "integration"; toolUse: ToolUseBlock; resolvedTool: ResolvedTool }
    29→  | { classification: "unknown"; toolUse: ToolUseBlock };
    30→
    31→/** Classification summary for all tool_use blocks in a response. */
    32→export type ToolRoutingResult = {
    33→  readonly classified: ClassifiedToolCall[];
    34→  readonly hasBrainNative: boolean;
    35→  readonly allUnknown: boolean;
    36→};
    37→
    38→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39→// Pure Function: classifyToolCalls
    40→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    41→
    42→/**
    43→ * Classify an array of tool_use blocks against the resolved toolset.
    44→ *
    45→ * - toolkit === "brain" -> brain-native (executed locally via graph queries)
    46→ * - toolkit present but not "brain" -> integration (needs credential brokerage)
    47→ * - not found in resolvedTools -> unknown (pass through to runtime)
    48→ */
    49→export function classifyToolCalls(
    50→  toolUseBlocks: ToolUseBlock[],
    51→  resolvedTools: ResolvedTool[],
    52→): ToolRoutingResult {
    53→  const toolsByName = new Map(resolvedTools.map((tool) => [tool.name, tool]));
    54→
    55→  const classified: ClassifiedToolCall[] = toolUseBlocks.map((toolUse) => {
    56→    const resolved = toolsByName.get(toolUse.name);
    57→
    58→    if (!resolved) {
    59→      return { classification: "unknown" as const, toolUse };
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    if (resolved.toolkit === "brain") {
    63→      return { classification: "brain-native" as const, toolUse, resolvedTool: resolved };
    64→    }
    65→
    66→    return { classification: "integration" as const, toolUse, resolvedTool: resolved };
    67→  });
    68→
    69→  const hasBrainNative = classified.some((c) => c.classification === "brain-native");
    70→  const allUnknown = classified.every((c) => c.classification === "unknown");
    71→
    72→  return { classified, hasBrainNative, allUnknown };
    73→}
    74→
    75→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    76→// Pure Helper: extractToolUseBlocks
    77→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    78→
    79→/** Extract tool_use content blocks from an Anthropic Messages API response body. */
    80→export function extractToolUseBlocks(
    81→  responseBody: Record<string, unknown>,
    82→): ToolUseBlock[] {
    83→  const content = responseBody.content as Array<Record<string, unknown>> | undefined;
    84→  if (!Array.isArray(content)) return [];
    85→
    86→  return content.filter(
    87→    (block): block is ToolUseBlock =>
    88→      block.type === "tool_use" &&
    89→      typeof block.id === "string" &&
    90→      typeof block.name === "string" &&
    91→      typeof block.input === "object" &&
    92→      block.input !== null,
    93→  );
    94→}
    95→
    96→/** Check if an Anthropic response indicates tool use (stop_reason === "tool_use"). */
    97→export function isToolUseResponse(responseBody: Record<string, unknown>): boolean {
    98→  return responseBody.stop_reason === "tool_use";
    99→}
   100→

```
     1→## Git Commits
     2→
     3→- Always use `--no-verify` when committing. The pre-commit hook requires `brain init` which is not available in worktree environments.
     4→- Always use `-s` (GPG sign) when committing.
     5→
     6→## Data Value Contract
     7→
     8→- Never persist, publish, or return `null` for domain data values (Surreal records, API payloads, events, UI state).
     9→- Absence must be represented by omitted optional fields only (`field?: Type`), not by `null`.
    10→- If `null` appears in domain data, treat it as a contract violation and fix the producer. Do NOT sanitize/coerce it at consumers.
```

    54→
    55→Tools live in `chat/tools/` as composable building blocks. Any agent (chat agent, PM subagent, future subagents) can compose the tools it needs. Key shared tools for work item management:
    56→
    57→| Tool | File | Purpose |
    58→|------|------|---------|
    59→| `suggest_work_items` | `chat/tools/suggest-work-items.ts` | Batch triage/dedup (>0.97 exact duplicate, ≥0.8 merge, <0.8 new) |
    60→| `create_work_item` | `chat/tools/create-work-item.ts` | Direct entity creation in graph |
    61→| `edit_work_item` | `chat/tools/edit-work-item.ts` | Edit existing task or feature |
    62→| `move_items_to_project` | `chat/tools/move-items-to-project.ts` | Move work items between projects |
    63→
    64→## Product Manager Subagent
    65→
    66→The PM agent (`agents/pm/`) is the single authority on tasks, features, and project status. It uses the AI SDK's `ToolLoopAgent` class and composes shared tools from `chat/tools/`. It is invoked by the chat agent via `invoke_pm_agent` tool with an intent:
    67→
    68→| Intent | When to use |

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     4→import {
     5→  listEntityNeighbors,
     6→  parseRecordIdString,
     7→  type GraphEntityTable,
     8→  type SearchEntityKind,
     9→} from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    11→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    12→
    13→export function createSearchEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    14→  return tool({
    15→    description:
    16→      "Full-text search across the knowledge graph. Use for finding entities by keyword or topic (e.g. \"authentication decisions\", \"payment tasks\"). For listing entities by kind or status, use list_workspace_entities instead.",
    17→    inputSchema: z.object({
    18→      query: z.string().min(1).describe("Search query (keywords matched via BM25 full-text search)"),
    19→      kinds: z.array(z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "suggestion"]))
    20→        .optional()
    21→        .describe("Optional filter by entity kinds"),
    22→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(10).describe("Maximum number of results"),
    23→    }),
    24→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    25→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    26→
    27→      const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    28→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    29→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    30→        query: input.query,
    31→        ...(input.kinds ? { kinds: input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[] } : {}),
    32→        limit: input.limit,
    33→      });
    34→
    35→      const enriched = await Promise.all(
    36→        results.map(async (row) => {
    37→          const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);
    38→          const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({
    39→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    40→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    41→            entityRecord,
    42→            limit: 8,
    43→          });
    44→
    45→          return {
    46→            id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,
    47→            kind: row.kind,
    48→            name: row.name,
    49→            confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),
    50→            ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),
    51→            related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({
    52→              id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,
    53→              kind: neighbor.kind,
    54→              name: neighbor.name,
    55→              relation: neighbor.relationKind,
    56→            })),
    57→          };
    58→        }),
    59→      );
    60→
    61→      return {
    62→        query: input.query,
    63→        results: enriched,
    64→      };
    65→    },
    66→  });
    67→}
    68→

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { z } from "zod";
     3→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
     4→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
     5→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
     6→
     7→function normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {
     8→  return new Set(
     9→    value
    10→      .toLowerCase()
    11→      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, " ")
    12→      .split(/\s+/)
    13→      .map((token) => token.trim())
    14→      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),
    15→  );
    16→}
    17→
    18→function hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {
    19→  for (const value of a) {
    20→    if (b.has(value)) {
    21→      return true;
    22→    }
    23→  }
    24→
    25→  return false;
    26→}
    27→
    28→export function createCheckConstraintsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    29→  return tool({
    30→    description:
    31→      "Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.",
    32→    inputSchema: z.object({
    33→      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe("What is being proposed"),
    34→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project scope"),
    35→    }),
    36→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    37→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    38→
    39→      const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
    40→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    41→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    42→        query: input.proposed_action,
    43→        kinds: ["decision"],
    44→        limit: 14,
    45→      });
    46→
    47→      const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(input.proposed_action);
    48→      const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    49→      const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    50→      const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];
    51→
    52→      for (const candidate of candidates) {
    53→        const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);
    54→        const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);
    55→        const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();
    56→
    57→        if (candidate.kind === "decision" && overlap && (status === "contested" || status === "superseded")) {
    58→          hardConflicts.push({
    59→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    60→            name: candidate.name,
    61→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    62→            reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,
    63→          });
    64→          continue;
    65→        }
    66→
    67→        if (candidate.kind === "decision" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {
    68→          supporting.push({
    69→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    70→            name: candidate.name,
    71→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    72→            reason: "High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.",
    73→          });
    74→          continue;
    75→        }
    76→
    77→        if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {
    78→          softTensions.push({
    79→            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,
    80→            name: candidate.name,
    81→            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),
    82→            reason: overlap
    83→              ? "Related decision/question may require consistency checks."
    84→              : "Semantically related context may be affected.",
    85→          });
    86→        }
    87→      }
    88→
    89→      return {
    90→        hard_conflicts: hardConflicts,
    91→        soft_tensions: softTensions,
    92→        supporting,
    93→        proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0,
    94→      };
    95→    },
    96→  });
    97→}
    98→
```

For listing entities by kind or status, use list_workspace_entities instead.\",\n    inputSchema: z.object({\n      query: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Search query (keywords matched via BM25 full-text search)\"),\n      kinds: z.array(z.enum([\"project\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"decision\", \"question\", \"suggestion\"]))\n        .optional()\n        .describe(\"Optional filter by entity kinds\"),\n      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(10).describe(\"Maximum number of results\"),\n    }),\n    execute: async (input, options) => {\n      const context = requireToolContext(options);\n\n      const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n        surreal: deps.surreal,\n        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,\n        query: input.query,\n        ...(input.kinds ? { kinds: input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[] } : {}),\n        limit: input.limit,\n      });\n\n      const enriched = await Promise.all(\n        results.map(async (row) => {\n          const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);\n          const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({\n            surreal: deps.surreal,\n            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,\n            entityRecord,\n            limit: 8,\n          });\n\n          return {\n            id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,\n            kind: row.kind,\n            name: row.name,\n            confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),\n            ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),\n            related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({\n              id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,\n              kind: neighbor.kind,\n              name: neighbor.name,\n              relation: neighbor.relationKind,\n            })),\n          };\n        }),\n      );\n\n      return {\n        query: input.query,\n        results: enriched,\n      };\n    },\n  });\n}",
  "new_string": "import { tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { RecordId, type Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from \"../graph/bm25-search\";\nimport {\n  listEntityNeighbors,\n  parseRecordIdString,\n  type GraphEntityTable,\n  type SearchEntityKind,\n} from \"../graph/queries\";\nimport { requireToolContext } from \"./helpers\";\nimport type { ChatToolDeps } from \"./types\";\n\n/** Core search logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */\nexport async function executeSearchEntities(\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n  input: { query: string; kinds?: SearchEntityKind[]; limit: number },\n) {\n  const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n    surreal,\n    workspaceRecord,\n    query: input.query,\n    ...(input.kinds ? { kinds: input.kinds } : {}),\n    limit: input.limit,\n  });\n\n  const enriched = await Promise.all(\n    results.map(async (row) => {\n      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);\n      const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({\n        surreal,\n        workspaceRecord,\n        entityRecord,\n        limit: 8,\n      });\n\n      return {\n        id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,\n        kind: row.kind,\n        name: row.name,\n        confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),\n        ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),\n        related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({\n          id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,\n          kind: neighbor.kind,\n          name: neighbor.name,\n          relation: neighbor.relationKind,\n        })),\n      };\n    }),\n  );\n\n  return { query: input.query, results: enriched };\n}\n\nexport function createSearchEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {\n  return tool({\n    description:\n      \"Full-text search across the knowledge graph. Use for finding entities by keyword or topic (e.g. \\\"authentication decisions\\\", \\\"payment tasks\\\"). For listing entities by kind or status, use list_workspace_entities instead.\",\n    inputSchema: z.object({\n      query: z.string().min(1).describe(\"Search query (keywords matched via BM25 full-text search)\"),\n      kinds: z.array(z.enum([\"project\", \"feature\", \"task\", \"decision\", \"question\", \"suggestion\"]))\n        .optional()\n        .describe(\"Optional filter by entity kinds\"),\n      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(25).default(10).describe(\"Maximum number of results\"),\n    }),\n    execute: async (input, options) => {\n      const context = requireToolContext(options);\n      return executeSearchEntities(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, {\n        query: input.query,\n        kinds: input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[] | undefined,\n        limit: input.limit,\n      });\n    },\n  });\n}"
}

     9→    description:

    15→      const context = requireToolContext(options);

    22→        surreal: deps.surreal,
    23→        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    24→        entityRecord,
    25→      });
    26→
    27→      return detail;
    28→    },
    29→  });
    30→}

     1→import { tool } from "ai";
     2→import { RecordId, Surreal } from "surrealdb";
     3→import { z } from "zod";
     4→import {
     5→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
     6→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
     7→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
     8→  resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord,
     9→} from "../graph/queries";
    10→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    11→import { requireToolContext } from "./helpers";
    12→import type { ChatToolDeps } from "./types";
    13→
    14→const entityKindEnum = z.enum(["project", "feature", "task", "decision", "question", "observation"]);
    15→
    16→export function createListWorkspaceEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {
    17→  return tool({
    18→    description:
    19→      "List workspace entities by kind. Use this to answer questions about what entities exist (e.g. \"what decisions are there?\", \"list open questions\", \"show all tasks\"). For semantic search by topic, use search_entities instead.",
    20→    inputSchema: z.object({
    21→      kind: entityKindEnum.describe("Entity kind to list."),
    22→      status: z.string().optional().describe("Optional status filter, e.g. 'provisional', 'confirmed', 'open', 'done'."),
    23→      project: z.string().optional().describe("Optional project name or ID to scope results. Only use when the user explicitly mentions a project."),
    24→      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(25).describe("Maximum number of results."),
    25→    }),
    26→    execute: async (input, options) => {
    27→      const context = requireToolContext(options);
    28→
    29→      const projectRecord = input.project
    30→        ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({
    31→            surreal: deps.surreal,
    32→            workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    33→            projectInput: input.project,
    34→          })
    35→        : undefined;
    36→
    37→      if (input.kind === "project") {
    38→        const projects = await listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({
    39→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    40→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    41→          limit: input.limit,
    42→        });
    43→        return {
    44→          kind: "project",
    45→          count: projects.length,
    46→          entities: projects.map((p) => ({
    47→            id: `project:${p.id}`,
    48→            name: p.name,
    49→            activeTaskCount: p.activeTaskCount,
    50→          })),
    51→        };
    52→      }
    53→
    54→      if (input.kind === "task") {
    55→        return listTasks(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    56→      }
    57→
    58→      if (input.kind === "feature") {
    59→        return listFeatures(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);
    60→      }
    61→
    62→      if (input.kind === "decision") {
    63→        const decisions = await listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({
    64→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    65→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    66→          limit: input.limit,
    67→        });
    68→        const filtered = input.status
    69→          ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === input.status)
    70→          : decisions;
    71→        return {
    72→          kind: "decision",
    73→          count: filtered.length,
    74→          entities: filtered.map((d) => ({
    75→            id: `decision:${d.id}`,
    76→            name: d.name,
    77→            status: d.status,
    78→            ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),
    79→            ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),
    80→          })),
    81→        };
    82→      }
    83→
    84→      if (input.kind === "question") {
    85→        const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({
    86→          surreal: deps.surreal,
    87→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
    88→          limit: input.limit,
    89→        });
    90→        return {
    91→          kind: "question",
    92→          count: questions.length,
    93→          entities: questions.map((q) => ({
    94→            id: `question:${q.id}`,
    95→            name: q.name,
    96→            ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),
    97→            ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),
    98→          })),
    99→        };
   100→      }
   101→
   102→      if (input.kind === "observation") {
   103→        const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({
   104→          surreal: deps.surreal,
   105→          workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,
   106→          limit: input.limit,
   107→        });
   108→        return {
   109→          kind: "observation",
   110→          count: observations.length,
   111→          entities: observations.map((o) => ({
   112→            id: `observation:${o.id}`,
   113→            text: o.text,
   114→            severity: o.severity,
   115→            status: o.status,
   116→            ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),
   117→            sourceAgent: o.sourceAgent,
   118→          })),
   119→        };
   120→      }
   121→
   122→      throw new Error(`unsupported entity kind: ${input.kind}`);
   123→    },
   124→  });
   125→}
   126→
   127→async function listTasks(
   128→  surreal: Surreal,
   129→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   130→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   131→  status: string | undefined,
   132→  limit: number,
   133→) {
   134→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   135→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   136→
   137→  if (projectRecord) {
   138→    conditions.push(
   139→      "(id IN (SELECT VALUE `in` FROM belongs_to WHERE out = $project)"
   140→      + " OR id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_task WHERE `in` IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)))",
   141→    );
   142→    params.project = projectRecord;
   143→  }
   144→
   145→  if (status) {
   146→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   147→    params.status = status;
   148→  }
   149→
   150→  const query = [
   151→    "SELECT id, title, status, priority, created_at",
   152→    "FROM task",
   153→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   154→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   155→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   156→  ].join(" ");
   157→
   158→  const [rows] = await surreal
   159→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   160→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"task", string>; title: string; status: string; priority?: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   161→
   162→  return {
   163→    kind: "task",
   164→    count: rows.length,
   165→    entities: rows.map((t) => ({
   166→      id: `task:${(t.id.id as string)}`,
   167→      name: t.title,
   168→      status: t.status,
   169→      ...(t.priority ? { priority: t.priority } : {}),
   170→    })),
   171→  };
   172→}
   173→
   174→async function listFeatures(
   175→  surreal: Surreal,
   176→  workspaceRecord: RecordId<"workspace", string>,
   177→  projectRecord: RecordId<"project", string> | undefined,
   178→  status: string | undefined,
   179→  limit: number,
   180→) {
   181→  const conditions = ["workspace = $workspace"];
   182→  const params: Record<string, unknown> = { workspace: workspaceRecord, limit };
   183→
   184→  if (projectRecord) {
   185→    conditions.push("id IN (SELECT VALUE out FROM has_feature WHERE `in` = $project)");
   186→    params.project = projectRecord;
   187→  }
   188→
   189→  if (status) {
   190→    conditions.push("status = $status");
   191→    params.status = status;
   192→  }
   193→
   194→  const query = [
   195→    "SELECT id, name, status, created_at",
   196→    "FROM feature",
   197→    `WHERE ${conditions.join(" AND ")}`,
   198→    "ORDER BY created_at DESC",
   199→    "LIMIT $limit;",
   200→  ].join(" ");
   201→
   202→  const [rows] = await surreal
   203→    .query<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>(query, params)
   204→    .collect<[Array<{ id: RecordId<"feature", string>; name: string; status: string; created_at: string | Date }>]>();
   205→
   206→  return {
   207→    kind: "feature",
   208→    count: rows.length,
   209→    entities: rows.map((f) => ({
   210→      id: `feature:${(f.id.id as string)}`,
   211→      name: f.name,
   212→      status: f.status,
   213→    })),
   214→  };
   215→}
   216→

  "new_string": "/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */\nexport async function executeListWorkspaceEntities(\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n  input: { kind: string; status?: string; project?: string; limit: number },\n) {\n  const projectRecord = input.project\n    ? await resolveWorkspaceProjectRecord({ surreal, workspaceRecord, projectInput: input.project })\n    : undefined;\n\n  if (input.kind === \"project\") {\n    const projects = await listWorkspaceProjectSummaries({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });\n    return {\n      kind: \"project\",\n      count: projects.length,\n      entities: projects.map((p) => ({ id: `project:${p.id}`, name: p.name, activeTaskCount: p.activeTaskCount })),\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (input.kind === \"task\") {\n    return listTasks(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);\n  }\n\n  if (input.kind === \"feature\") {\n    return listFeatures(surreal, workspaceRecord, projectRecord, input.status, input.limit);\n  }\n\n  if (input.kind === \"decision\") {\n    const decisions = await listWorkspaceRecentDecisions({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });\n    const filtered = input.status ? decisions.filter((d) => d.status === input.status) : decisions;\n    return {\n      kind: \"decision\",\n      count: filtered.length,\n      entities: filtered.map((d) => ({\n        id: `decision:${d.id}`, name: d.name, status: d.status,\n        ...(d.priority ? { priority: d.priority } : {}),\n        ...(d.project ? { project: d.project } : {}),\n      })),\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (input.kind === \"question\") {\n    const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });\n    return {\n      kind: \"question\",\n      count: questions.length,\n      entities: questions.map((q) => ({\n        id: `question:${q.id}`, name: q.name,\n        ...(q.priority ? { priority: q.priority } : {}),\n        ...(q.project ? { project: q.project } : {}),\n      })),\n    };\n  }\n\n  if (input.kind === \"observation\") {\n    const observations = await listWorkspaceOpenObservations({ surreal, workspaceRecord, limit: input.limit });\n    return {\n      kind: \"observation\",\n      count: observations.length,\n      entities: observations.map((o) => ({\n        id: `observation:${o.id}`, text: o.text, severity: o.severity, status: o.status,\n        ...(o.category ? { category: o.category } : {}),\n        sourceAgent: o.sourceAgent,\n      })),\n    };\n  }\n\n  throw new Error(`unsupported entity kind: ${input.kind}`);\n}\n\nexport function createListWorkspaceEntitiesTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {\n  return tool({\n    description:\n      \"List workspace entities by kind. Use this to answer questions about what entities exist (e.g. \\\"what decisions are there?\\\", \\\"list open questions\\\", \\\"show all tasks\\\"). For semantic search by topic, use search_entities instead.\",\n    inputSchema: z.object({\n      kind: entityKindEnum.describe(\"Entity kind to list.\"),\n      status: z.string().optional().describe(\"Optional status filter, e.g. 'provisional', 'confirmed', 'open', 'done'.\"),\n      project: z.string().optional().describe(\"Optional project name or ID to scope results. Only use when the user explicitly mentions a project.\"),\n      limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(50).default(25).describe(\"Maximum number of results.\"),\n    }),\n    execute: async (input, options) => {\n      const context = requireToolContext(options);\n      return executeListWorkspaceEntities(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, {\n        kind: input.kind,\n        status: input.status,\n        project: input.project,\n        limit: input.limit,\n      });\n    },\n  });\n}"
}

  "old_string": "import { tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from \"../graph/bm25-search\";\nimport { requireToolContext } from \"./helpers\";\nimport type { ChatToolDeps } from \"./types\";\n\nfunction normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {\n  return new Set(\n    value\n      .toLowerCase()\n      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\\s]/g, \" \")\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .map((token) => token.trim())\n      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {\n  for (const value of a) {\n    if (b.has(value)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return false;\n}\n\nexport function createCheckConstraintsTool(deps: ChatToolDeps) {\n  return tool({\n    description:\n      \"Check if a proposed action conflicts with existing decisions or constraints. Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.\",\n    inputSchema: z.object({\n      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe(\"What is being proposed\"),\n      project: z.string().optional().describe(\"Optional project scope\"),\n    }),\n    execute: async (input, options) => {\n      const context = requireToolContext(options);\n\n      const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n        surreal: deps.surreal,\n        workspaceRecord: context.workspaceRecord,\n        query: input.proposed_action,\n        kinds: [\"decision\"],\n        limit: 14,\n      });\n\n      const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(input.proposed_action);\n      const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n      const softTensions: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n      const supporting: Array<{ id: string; name: string; score: number; reason: string }> = [];\n\n      for (const candidate of candidates) {\n        const candidateTokens = normalizeTokens(candidate.name);\n        const overlap = hasTokenOverlap(actionTokens, candidateTokens);\n        const status = candidate.status?.toLowerCase();\n\n        if (candidate.kind === \"decision\" && overlap && (status === \"contested\" || status === \"superseded\")) {\n          hardConflicts.push({\n            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,\n            name: candidate.name,\n            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n            reason: `Decision is marked ${status}.`,\n          });\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        if (candidate.kind === \"decision\" && candidate.score >= 0.86 && overlap) {\n          supporting.push({\n            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,\n            name: candidate.name,\n            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n            reason: \"High-similarity decision aligns with proposed action.\",\n          });\n          continue;\n        }\n\n        if (candidate.score >= 0.72) {\n          softTensions.push({\n            id: `${candidate.kind}:${candidate.id}`,\n            name: candidate.name,\n            score: Number(candidate.score.toFixed(4)),\n            reason: overlap\n              ? \"Related decision/question may require consistency checks.\"\n              : \"Semantically related context may be affected.\",\n          });\n        }\n      }\n\n      return {\n        hard_conflicts: hardConflicts,\n        soft_tensions: softTensions,\n        supporting,\n        proceed: hardConflicts.length === 0,\n      };\n    },\n  });\n}",
  "new_string": "import { tool } from \"ai\";\nimport { RecordId, type Surreal } from \"surrealdb\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from \"../graph/bm25-search\";\nimport { requireToolContext } from \"./helpers\";\nimport type { ChatToolDeps } from \"./types\";\n\nfunction normalizeTokens(value: string): Set<string> {\n  return new Set(\n    value\n      .toLowerCase()\n      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\\s]/g, \" \")\n      .split(/\\s+/)\n      .map((token) => token.trim())\n      .filter((token) => token.length > 2),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction hasTokenOverlap(a: Set<string>, b: Set<string>): boolean {\n  for (const value of a) {\n    if (b.has(value)) return true;\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** Core logic — shared by AI SDK tool wrapper and proxy handler. */\nexport async function executeCheckConstraints(\n  surreal: Surreal,\n  workspaceRecord: RecordId<\"workspace\", string>,\n  proposedAction: string,\n) {\n  const candidates = await searchEntitiesByBm25({\n    surreal,\n    workspaceRecord,\n    query: proposedAction,\n    kinds: [\"decision\"],\n    limit: 14,\n  });\n\n  const actionTokens = normalizeTokens(proposedAction);\n  const hardConflicts: Array<{ id: string; Returns hard conflicts, soft tensions, supporting context, and proceed flag.\",\n    inputSchema: z.object({\n      proposed_action: z.string().min(1).describe(\"What is being proposed\"),\n      project: z.string().optional().describe(\"Optional project scope\"),\n    }),\n    execute: async (input, options) => {\n      const context = requireToolContext(options);\n      return executeCheckConstraints(deps.surreal, context.workspaceRecord, input.proposed_action);\n    },\n  });\n}"

     1→/**
     2→ * Tool Executor -- Effect Boundary
     3→ *
     4→ * Executes Brain-native tool calls via graph queries. This is the adapter/effect
     5→ * boundary -- it performs IO (SurrealDB queries) to fulfill tool calls that the
     6→ * pure tool-router classified as "brain-native".
     7→ *
     8→ * Each Brain-native tool handler is a function: (input, deps) -> ToolExecutionResult.
     9→ * Unknown tool names produce an error result (not an exception).
    10→ *
    11→ * All handlers delegate to the shared graph query layer (graph/, observation/)
    12→ * rather than reimplementing queries inline.
    13→ *
    14→ * Step 8.5 in the proxy pipeline.
    15→ */
    16→import { RecordId, type Surreal } from "surrealdb";
    17→import type { ClassifiedToolCall } from "./tool-router";
    18→import { log } from "../telemetry/logger";
    19→import { captureToolTrace } from "./tool-trace-writer";
    20→import {
    21→  resolveCredentialsForTool,
    22→  type CredentialResolverDeps,
    23→} from "./credential-resolver";
    24→import {
    25→  fetchGovernancePolicies,
    26→  countTodayToolExecutions,
    27→  fetchCanUseRateLimit,
    28→  countHourlyToolExecutions,
    29→  type GovernsPolicyRow,
    30→} from "../tool-registry/queries";
    31→import { searchEntitiesByBm25 } from "../graph/bm25-search";
    32→import {
    33→  getEntityDetail,
    34→  getProjectStatus,
    35→  listEntityNeighbors,
    36→  listWorkspaceProjectSummaries,
    37→  listWorkspaceRecentDecisions,
    38→  listWorkspaceOpenQuestions,
    39→  parseRecordIdString,
    40→  type GraphEntityTable,
    41→  type SearchEntityKind,
    42→} from "../graph/queries";
    43→import { listWorkspaceOpenObservations } from "../observation/queries";
    44→
    45→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→// Types
    47→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    48→
    49→/** Result of executing a single Brain-native tool call. */
    50→export type ToolExecutionResult = {
    51→  readonly toolUseId: string;
    52→  readonly content: string;
    53→  readonly isError: boolean;
    54→};
    55→
    56→/** Dependencies injected into tool executors. */
    57→export type ToolExecutorDeps = {
    58→  readonly surreal: Surreal;
    59→  readonly workspaceId: string;
    60→  readonly identityId?: string;
    61→  readonly sessionId?: string;
    62→};
    63→
    64→/** Dependencies for integration tool execution (extends base with credential resolver). */
    65→export type IntegrationExecutorDeps = ToolExecutorDeps & {
    66→  readonly toolEncryptionKey: string;
    67→};
    68→
    69→/** Anthropic tool_result content block for follow-up requests. */
    70→export type ToolResultMessage = {
    71→  role: "user";
    72→  content: Array<{
    73→    type: "tool_result";
    74→    tool_use_id: string;
    75→    content: string;
    76→    is_error?: boolean;
    77→  }>;
    78→};
    79→
    80→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    81→// Governance Types (Pure)
    82→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    83→
    84→/** Result of evaluating governance policies for a tool call. */
    85→export type GovernanceVerdict =
    86→  | { readonly allowed: true }
    87→  | { readonly allowed: false; readonly reason: string; readonly outcome: "denied" | "rate_limited" };
    88→
    89→/**
    90→ * Evaluate governance policies (pure function).
    91→ *
    92→ * Rules:
    93→ *   - "requires_human_approval" condition -> denied
    94→ *   - max_per_day exceeded -> denied
    95→ *   - Multiple policies: most restrictive wins (any denial = denied)
    96→ */
    97→export function evaluateGovernancePolicies(
    98→  policies: GovernsPolicyRow[],
    99→  todayExecutionCount: number,
   100→): GovernanceVerdict {
   101→  for (const policy of policies) {
   102→    // Check requires_human_approval condition
   103→    if (policy.conditions === "requires_human_approval") {
   104→      return {
   105→        allowed: false,
   106→        reason: `Tool call denied by policy "${policy.policyTitle}": requires human approval`,
   107→        outcome: "denied",
   108→      };
   109→    }
   110→
   111→    // Check max_per_day limit
   112→    if (policy.max_per_day !== undefined && todayExecutionCount >= policy.max_per_day) {
   113→      return {
   114→        allowed: false,
   115→        reason: `Tool call denied by policy "${policy.policyTitle}": daily limit of ${policy.max_per_day} exceeded (${todayExecutionCount} calls today)`,
   116→        outcome: "denied",
   117→      };
   118→    }
   119→  }
   120→
   121→  return { allowed: true };
   122→}
   123→
   124→/**
   125→ * Evaluate can_use rate limit (pure function).
   126→ */
   127→export function evaluateRateLimit(
   128→  maxCallsPerHour: number | undefined,
   129→  hourlyExecutionCount: number,
   130→): GovernanceVerdict {
   131→  if (maxCallsPerHour !== undefined && hourlyExecutionCount >= maxCallsPerHour) {
   132→    return {
   133→      allowed: false,
   134→      reason: `Rate limit exceeded: ${hourlyExecutionCount}/${maxCallsPerHour} calls per hour`,
   135→      outcome: "rate_limited",
   136→    };
   137→  }
   138→
   139→  return { allowed: true };
   140→}
   141→
   142→/** Handler for a single Brain-native tool. */
   143→type BrainToolHandler = (
   144→  input: Record<string, unknown>,
   145→  deps: ToolExecutorDeps,
   146→) => Promise<ToolExecutionResult>;
   147→
   148→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   149→// Helpers
   150→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   151→
   152→function wsRecord(deps: ToolExecutorDeps): RecordId<"workspace", string> {
   153→  return new RecordId("workspace", deps.workspaceId);
   154→}
   155→
   156→function ok(content: unknown): ToolExecutionResult {
   157→  return { toolUseId: "", content: JSON.stringify(content), isError: false };
   158→}
   159→
   160→function fail(message: string): ToolExecutionResult {
   161→  return { toolUseId: "", content: JSON.stringify({ error: message }), isError: true };
   162→}
   163→
   164→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   165→// Brain-Native Tool Handlers
   166→//
   167→// All handlers delegate to the shared graph query layer (graph/, observation/)
   168→// rather than reimplementing queries inline.
   169→// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   170→

   170→
   171→const searchEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
   172→  const query = typeof input.query === "string" ? input.query : "";
   173→  if (!query.trim()) return ok({ results: [], message: "Empty search query" });
   174→
   175→  const kinds = Array.isArray(input.kinds) ? (input.kinds as SearchEntityKind[]) : undefined;
   176→  const limit = typeof input.limit === "number" ? Math.min(input.limit, 25) : 10;
   177→
   178→  const results = await searchEntitiesByBm25({
   179→    surreal: deps.surreal,
   180→    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),
   181→    query,
   182→    ...(kinds ? { kinds } : {}),
   183→    limit,
   184→  });
   185→
   186→  const enriched = await Promise.all(
   187→    results.map(async (row) => {
   188→      const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(row.id, [row.kind as GraphEntityTable], row.kind as GraphEntityTable);
   189→      const neighbors = await listEntityNeighbors({
   190→        surreal: deps.surreal,
   191→        workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),
   192→        entityRecord,
   193→        limit: 8,
   194→      });
   195→
   196→      return {
   197→        id: `${row.kind}:${row.id}`,
   198→        kind: row.kind,
   199→        name: row.name,
   200→        confidence: Number(row.score.toFixed(4)),
   201→        ...(row.status ? { status: row.status } : {}),
   202→        related: neighbors.slice(0, 4).map((neighbor) => ({
   203→          id: `${neighbor.kind}:${neighbor.id}`,
   204→          kind: neighbor.kind,
   205→          name: neighbor.name,
   206→          relation: neighbor.relationKind,
   207→        })),
   208→      };
   209→    }),
   210→  );
   211→
   212→  return ok({ query, results: enriched });
   213→};
   214→
   215→const getEntityDetailHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
   216→  const entityId = typeof input.entityId === "string" ? input.entityId : "";
   217→  if (!entityId.trim()) return fail("entityId is required");
   218→
   219→  const entityRecord = parseRecordIdString(
   220→    entityId,
   221→    ["workspace", "project", "person", "feature", "task", "decision", "question"],
   222→  ) as unknown as ReturnType<typeof parseRecordIdString<GraphEntityTable>>;
   223→
   224→  const detail = await getEntityDetail({
   225→    surreal: deps.surreal,
   226→    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),
   227→    entityRecord,
   228→  });
   229→
   230→  return ok(detail);
   231→};
   232→
   233→const getProjectStatusHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
   234→  const projectId = typeof input.projectId === "string" ? input.projectId : "";
   235→  if (!projectId.trim()) return fail("projectId is required");
   236→
   237→  const status = await getProjectStatus({
   238→    surreal: deps.surreal,
   239→    workspaceRecord: wsRecord(deps),
   240→    projectInput: projectId,
   241→  });
   242→
   243→  return ok(status);
   244→};
   245→
   246→const listWorkspaceEntitiesHandler: BrainToolHandler = async (input, deps) => {
   247→  const kind = typeof input.kind === "string" ? input.kind : "";
   248→  const limit = typeof input.limit === "number" ? Math.min(input.limit, 50) : 25;

   257→    });

   264→    return ok({
   265→      kind: "decision",

   272→    });
   273→  }
   274→
   275→  if (kind === "question") {
   276→    const questions = await listWorkspaceOpenQuestions({ surreal: deps.surreal, workspaceRecord: ws, limit });

Only the `SearchEntityKind` type import remains — used by the `searchEntitiesHandler` to cast `input.kinds`. All the direct graph/observation imports are gone.

Here's the final state:

**What changed:**
1. **Moved** `chat/tools/` → `tools/` (all imports updated, 15+ consumer files)
2. **Extracted** `execute*` functions from 5 tool files (`search-entities`, `get-entity-detail`, `get-project-status`, `list-workspace-entities`, `check-constraints`) — standalone async functions that take `(surreal, workspaceRecord, input)` 
3. **AI SDK wrappers** now call these `execute*` functions after extracting context from `requireToolContext(options)`
4. **Proxy handlers** are thin adapters (~3 lines) that validate input and delegate to the same `execute*` functions — no duplicated business logic
5. **Removed** ~200 lines of duplicated code from `tool-executor.ts` (inline queries, `normalizeTokens`, `hasTokenOverlap`, etc.)
